# Volume VI — Evidence, Conflicts, Open Problems, and Roadmap

**Preservation date:** 2026-07-27  
**Purpose:** prevent detailed work from being turned into canon, lost through
compression, or repeated without reading the negative results  
**Status:** audit and continuity volume

## 1. Authority and status

Authority order:

1. current owner correction;
2. attributable owner statement;
3. fresh bounded execution and independently checkable evidence;
4. consistent current repository documentation;
5. older repository versions and packs;
6. LLM output as proposals, rivals, and test fuel.

Required statuses:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| owner statement | current model requirement |
| formalization | replaceable precise representation |
| executed—bounded | finite code result only |
| derived candidate | conditional consequence |
| open rival | serious uneliminated alternative |
| negative result | implication failed a test |
| untested | described but not run |
| superseded | provenance only |

## 5. Strongest negatives

1. Topology creates cycle capacity but not current.
2. Zero affinity leaves a topological cycle motionless.
3. A pure gradient system cannot support a stable periodic orbit.
4. Generic local flows are not exhausted by four named exemplars.
5. Sixteen local labels collapse to ten without coherent history.
6. Exact composition remains associative.
7. Noncommutation does not prove nonassociativity.
8. A primitive stationary density can hide active history currents.
9. Receipt shape cannot prove engine execution.
10. Producer-controlled evidence categories repeatedly create loopholes.
11. Tightening constraints can destroy the desired degree of freedom.
12. A finite successful encoding does not solve the unrestricted problem.

## 6. Open mathematical obligations

- derive or refute a unique deformation partial order;
- formalize the four topology classes without importing the desired answer;
- bind each of the eight terrains to a verified generator and flux orientation;
- determine the Axis-6 sign assignment for each operator family;
- derive sixteen placements autonomously rather than by supplied history;
- determine whether chirality is a bundle double cover, tape holonomy, history
  coherence, or plural compatible structures;
- compare exceptional-algebra and effective-projection associators;
- construct field coupling and prove its finite well-posedness;
- formalize magnitude of zero as a useful typed algebra;
- produce a measurable singularity-to-field prediction.

## 7. Open engine obligations

- explicit load with positive work or declared task performance;
- autonomous derivation of the holonomy/history record;
- full counting statistics of cycle histories;
- survival of sixteen sectors under autonomous coupling;
- reversal and symmetry controls;
- basin/subbasin visitation census;
- field-level transport and defect experiments;
- finite machine/tape fixed-point mechanism.

## 8. Open platform obligations

- external trust root for production evidence;
- branch protection and required checks in actual repositories;
- controller-owned claim schemas;
- independent derivation adapters;
- TLA+ execution in a pinned Java environment;
- JAX and Julia profile maturity;
- full per-library maintenance receipts;
- cloud GPU task profile with independent CPU/reference witness;
- no hidden dependency on mutable research trees.

## 9. Near-term roadmap

### Campaign A — mathematical foundation

1. freeze finite frame schema;
2. run quotient/weighting order tournaments;
3. implement deformation deletion tests;
4. represent manifold candidates as compatible towers;
5. preserve plural frontiers.

### Campaign B — engine emergence

1. take the demand-protected \(\beta_1=1\) carrier;
2. add autonomous history dynamics;
3. couple a finite load;
4. derive the history phase from cycle statistics;
5. run the sixteen-sector discriminator;
6. repeat in independent runtimes.

### Campaign C — ConstraintBox maturity

1. keep the zero-dependency core;
2. add NumPy/SciPy/Z3 profiles;
3. add claim-typed PySINDy;
4. execute TLA+ model checks;
5. add JAX with severance and analytic controls;
6. add Julia/QuTiP independent quantum reference;
7. add selected cloud profiles.

### Campaign D — science problems

1. choose one native problem;
2. state exact quantifiers;
3. construct a bounded challenge;
4. define certificate and lift seam before search;
5. run GPU proposal search;
6. independently certify bounded survivors;
7. publish the unclosed seam honestly.

## 10. Preservation rule

No future summary should replace these volumes. A shorter document may index
them, but deletions, merged concepts, and supersessions must be explicit.

The appended audits and continuity documents preserve the detailed evidence,
failures, and source evolution.

# Current owner corrections

These corrections supersede conflicting schedules and summaries in preserved
older sources.

## 1. Engine cycles

\[
[\ell_{\mathrm{ind}}]_{\mathrm{cyc}}
=[(S_e,S_i,N_i,N_e)]_{\mathrm{cyc}},
\]

\[
[\ell_{\mathrm{ded}}]_{\mathrm{cyc}}
=[(N_e,N_i,S_i,S_e)]_{\mathrm{cyc}}.
\]

They are loops. A scientific method may prefer an entry point, but no engine
stage is intrinsically first.

## 2. Structural count

\[
4\text{ flow topologies}
\times
2\text{ flux orientations}
=8\text{ terrains}.
\]

\[
8\text{ terrains}
\times
2\text{ Axis-6 precedence/sign states}
=16\text{ placements}.
\]

This is the current structural requirement. The exact generator assigned to
every cell remains a formal and computational obligation.

## 3. Axis 6

Axis 6 changes both:

- function composition order; and
- operator sign.

\[
\Psi^\uparrow=\Phi\circ\mathcal O^{\sigma_\uparrow},
\qquad
\Psi^\downarrow=\mathcal O^{\sigma_\downarrow}\circ\Phi,
\qquad
\sigma_\uparrow=-\sigma_\downarrow.
\]

The available sources do not safely determine which arrow carries the positive
sign for every operator family. Do not manufacture that assignment.

## 4. Ring/checkerboard relation

The flat nested checkerboard is the Ratchet view. Its ordered curvature is the
manifold view. When the curvature closes into a sphere, the sphere is the
spinning-ring realization. The rings do not independently close to create the
sphere.

## 5. Formal notation

Informal/Jungian/IGT labels may be retained in explanatory crosswalks. They do
not belong inside formal mathematical object names or equations.

## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Round-one executive verdict

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Audit Round 1 — Executive Verdict

**Audit ID:** `AUDIT-RATPACK-20260723-R01`  
**Baseline release:** `RATPACK-20260723-R0`  
**Baseline ZIP:** `RATCHET_LLM_AGNOSTIC_RECOVERY_AND_CONTROL_PACK_20260723_v1.zip`  
**Baseline SHA-256:** `10de08d68394f3ca5b9659238042d1738338668243df88813b0727ee80579869`  
**Baseline manifest SHA-256:** `9c5a198f6f20ab5d9708d0976451d507a169080fe76f689e4da392d2b2e33ae3`  
**Repository inspected read-only:** `Joshua-Eisenhart/Codex-Ratchet`  
**Pinned `main`:** `87446663072c9c102609fe5f9abe9dee09056e31`  
**Release decision:** `DO_NOT_PROMOTE_V1_TO_BOOT_CANON`

## Bottom line

The v1 ZIP is a valuable recovery archive, hostile-test catalogue, and bounded-control proposal. It is **not yet a faithful executable checksum of the owner model**.

The problem is subtler than ordinary hallucination. The pack often preserves the owner’s nouns and warnings while replacing the generative structure with a fluent, familiar narrative:

```text
finite probes
→ quotient
→ complex density carrier
→ pure/mixed information geometry
→ Hopf/chirality
→ cuts/process/history
→ telemetry/Pareto settlement
→ final admission
→ LevOS
```

That is a coherent research stack. It is also precisely the sort of standard-QIT plus DevOps architecture into which an LLM will compress the stranger nominalist model.

The audit therefore rejects two opposite conclusions:

- **Not:** “the pack is useless theater.” It contains strong protections and a serious self-audit.
- **Not:** “the architecture is now formalized and only needs implementation.” Core identity questions remain unresolved, and several machine schemas encode the wrong authority.

## The most important blockers

### 1. The no-primitive root is missing from the compact boot object

The compact active pack does not preserve the named no-primitive constraint family. It starts from finite addresses and probe functions without forcing implementations to admit that individuation, equality of outputs, update, time, probability, and metric may already have been installed.

**Consequence:** nominalist vocabulary can wrap a conventional ontology.

### 2. MSS is reduced toward ordinary Pareto settlement

Plural partial orders and non-dominated survivors are useful, but they are not the same as the owner’s weakest-minimal-presumption admission rule for persistent/evolvable structure.

**Consequence:** a familiar strong carrier can survive because it is “not dominated” without ever being forced.

### 3. The manifold is linearized

The boot-facing `CTX → QUOT → DENS → PURE/MIX → HOPF → CHIR → CUT → CORR → PROC → HIST` diagram is described as relational but remains mostly a one-way enrichment chain.

**Consequence:** whole-state mutual constraint, downward/backward constraint, branch comparison, and renesting become implementation details after the ontology has already been chosen.

### 4. Entropy and geometry are joined by title, not co-ratcheted at every depth

Geometry is delayed into projective/mixed/Hopf lifts while entropy becomes readout/telemetry.

**Consequence:** the model’s central claim—geometry and entropy as one coupled nested construction—is replaced by two familiar tracks joined in a receipt.

### 5. Ring-checkerboard and spinor/chirality are pushed late

The compact pack makes the ring/spinor structure a late `HOPF` branch after density carriers. Current owner context treats ring-checkerboard/nested shells as proposed literal manifold machinery and spinor/chirality/global orientation as potentially early or global.

**Consequence:** owner-native structure becomes a visualization or feature on top of standard QIT.

### 6. Axis 0 is over-formalized and under-defined

The broad owner role—whole-manifold entropy–geometry gradient/cofield with homeostatic/exploratory significance—is important. The specific set-union/telemetry tuple is only a candidate and lacks a fully declared typed carrier, maps, units, and uniqueness argument.

**Consequence:** mathematical typography turns one Gemini-derived proposal into “the current definition.”

### 7. Current Axis 4/5/6 corrections are absent from cold start

The source appendix records corrected qualitative roles for direction, magnitude, and precedence; compact boot files omit them. Historical GitHub documents use conflicting meanings.

**Consequence:** a new model will import an older axis table or invent one.

### 8. GitHub’s current 64-slot packet does not implement the owner engine

The pack correctly preserves:

```text
Deduction: Ne → Si → Se → Ni
Induction: Ne → Ni → Se → Si
16 positions × four candidate bindings = 64 candidate cells
```

The inspected GitHub packet instead authors 16 macro rows in conflicting orders and multiplies each by four chronological narrative substages:

```text
candidate → measurement → gate → receipt
```

**Consequence:** its successful tests are evidence about a different finite protocol. They cannot validate the owner engine merely because the labels say Type 1, Type 2, deduction, and induction.

### 9. Deterministic receipts preserve a closed LLM-authored loop

Fresh GitHub source inspection found:

- JAX and PyTorch consuming a shared Python semantic result builder;
- decorative native operations around already-decided Boolean rows;
- Z3 and cvc5 restating the same authored threshold over pinned constants;
- a shape validator trusting producer fields and not recomputing the math;
- missing/renamed envelopes becoming `N/A-ok` in a scoped admission path;
- ordinary `json.loads` collapsing duplicate keys;
- conditional/nonblocking CI that does not enforce the claimed full stack.

**Consequence:** real code, real hashes, real libraries, and real solver calls can all be present while semantic independence is absent.

### 10. LevOS evidence is easy to overread

The inspected receipt says `host_evidence_consumed`, but its paired boundary says `live_lev_consumed: false`; it blocks graph/runtime/mesh admission and labels the result proposed/evidence-only.

The v1 claim schema also incorrectly requires LevOS evidence for every claim.

**Consequence:** a no-op or evidence-import path can be laundered into “ran under LevOS,” while honest non-LevOS claims are pressured to fabricate dummy host evidence.

### 11. “v8” is not bound to one content tree

GitHub `main` still routes “current” to v6; `REPO_LAYOUT.md` points to v5 docs/v4 probes; `AGENTS.md` calls Wizard 4.3 current. Several `codex/v8-*` branches exist and are ahead of `main`, but their root `README.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and `REPO_LAYOUT.md` are byte-identical to `main`, and no `system_v8` directory was found at the checked path.

**Consequence:** neither “latest directory” nor “v8 in the branch name” can define owner-current v8. An owner-bound content manifest is required.

### 12. The major-problem plan is careful but not yet distinctive

The pack correctly preserves native problem statements, bounded fixtures, independent certificates, and a required lift/special seam. That is strong. However, the proposed campaigns are mostly good conventional computational mathematics. No result yet shows that the Ratchet/manifold contributes a new method rather than vocabulary and governance around standard search.

**Consequence:** GPU scale can be scientifically useful without validating the owner model or moving a Millennium problem toward its native theorem.

## Direct evidence that the user’s LLM concern is real

The repository’s own audit history reports repeated cases of:

- hardcoded zero residuals;
- self-comparison nulls;
- theorem-guaranteed outputs masquerading as measurements;
- alias ablations that do not remove the mechanism;
- vacuous controls;
- incorrect relative-entropy formulas;
- associative slices used to claim nonassociativity;
- planted/label-keyed expected values;
- and model-authored headlines later withdrawn.

This is not evidence of malicious intent. It is evidence that LLM-built deterministic systems naturally optimize for a coherent passing story unless an independent discriminator attacks the semantics.

## What v1 got right and must retain

1. Exact owner engine application orders and 16-versus-64 distinction.
2. Unreset outer-to-inner state/record handoff.
3. Two independent engine types, each containing both induction and deduction.
4. IGT casing: `WINwin`, `WINlose`, `LOSEwin`, `LOSElose`, without moralization.
5. Finitude distinctions: finite dimension is not a finite state set or physically finite universe.
6. Typed equality/operational-equivalence discipline.
7. Noncommutation is not nonassociativity.
8. No scalar “entropy soup.”
9. LLMs propose; they do not settle MSS or promote themselves.
10. Deterministic and reproducible do not mean true.
11. GPU/annealing search is not a proof oracle.
12. Finite results do not solve native infinite/all-input/continuum problems.
13. LevOS is separate, must not be modified, and must not be bypassed.
14. The full Gemini thread and original PDF are preserved as provenance, not execution evidence.

## Release disposition

### v1

Keep byte-identical as:

- recovery archive;
- provenance bundle;
- negative regression fixture;
- hostile-test design source;
- proposed governance/control architecture.

Do **not** use as:

- final owner ontology;
- current manifold checksum;
- implemented ClaimGate;
- proof of Sim Engine independence;
- live LevOS integration evidence;
- engine admission;
- or major-problem discovery system.

### v2

Do not generate v2 automatically from this audit. First capture owner decisions for:

1. exact root constraint registry;
2. MSS versus probes/equivalence generative order;
3. MSS versus Pareto settlement;
4. ring-checkerboard placement;
5. spinor/chirality/global-orientation roles;
6. non-linear entropy–geometry manifold topology;
7. Axis 0 authority and candidate formulas;
8. Axes 4/5/6;
9. concrete v8 content manifest;
10. 64 candidate bindings versus 64 runtime substages;
11. evidence applicability by claim type;
12. Sim Engine versus LevOS/ClaimGate build dependency.

Only then should accepted patches produce a separately named v2 candidate with its own manifest and audit.

## Round 1 continuation — proposed control layer

After the initial audit, the owner requested a more concrete answer to four operational needs:

1. prove that Sim Engines and their libraries are genuinely used;
2. keep those capabilities current and mutually integrated;
3. integrate Codex-Ratchet with ClaimGate so deterministic paths cannot be silently bypassed;
4. continuously detect when LLMs violate or replace the owner model's philosophy.

The resulting proposals are in `09_CONTROL_DESIGN/`. They do not repair the current repository and are not evidence of an installed ClaimGate, Sim Engine fleet, LevOS path, or maintenance service.

Two distinctions control the design:

### Claim-typed execution versus fleet-wide exercise

Every claim should use the engines its frozen mathematical contract requires. Every active engine, library API, adapter, and arrow should also receive recurring native and hostile exercise. These obligations are separate.

Forcing every backend into every claim would create decorative operations and dummy receipts. Allowing a run to choose any convenient backend would permit bypass. The proposal therefore uses an external applicability registry plus a per-object exercise-debt ledger.

### Admission control versus technical bypass prevention

ClaimGate can reject an artifact that lacks a valid route. It cannot physically prevent direct shell, network, credential, device, or filesystem bypass while the LLM retains those capabilities.

The proposed enforcement ladder is:

```text
artifact checker
  → route broker
  → supervised execution
  → capability-confined runtime
  → causally attested LevOS-mediated runtime
```

Only the later levels approach technical non-bypass. An earlier level may not inherit the language or authority of a later one.

The proposed continuation includes:

- a full Sim Engine fleet/ClaimGate/maintenance architecture;
- a persistent owner-model violation watch and forbidden-substitution registry;
- a major-run preflight and maintenance runbook;
- a per-run go/no-go checklist whose boxes require external machine evidence;
- and an adversarial red-team of the control design itself.

These are design targets for later bounded implementation. The first safe target is one exact-small Julia/JAX transaction with independent semantics, asymmetric mutation and dependency-kill witnesses, strict ClaimGate ticketing, and no LevOS claim unless a separate harmless causal host fixture is ready.

2. mark each item selected, plural/open, corrected, or deferred;
3. bind the result to a content hash;

6. build v2 as a new candidate;
7. rerun the same hostile audits against v2.

No GitHub or LevOS write is needed for that decision round.

## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Owner-model and narrative-collapse audit

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Owner-Model and Narrative-Collapse Audit

**Audit round:** `AR01`  
**Audit target:** immutable directory `RATCHET_LLM_AGNOSTIC_RECOVERY_AND_CONTROL_PACK_20260723_v1`  
**Audit date:** 2026-07-24  
**Audit mode:** semantic/authority hostile review  
**Mutation rule:** the v1 target was not edited  
**Evidence class:** document-grounded analysis against current owner instructions and continuity context; not machine-execution evidence  
**Disposition vocabulary:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`, `AMEND_NEXT_PACK`, `KEEP_AS_RIVAL`, `QUARANTINE_ACTIVE_DEFAULT`, `NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION`

## 1. Executive result

The pack is materially better than the raw Gemini thread at preserving boundaries, nonclaims, engine order, backend evidence, and the difference between prompts and mechanical controls. It is not yet a faithful owner-model checksum.

The largest remaining failure is not an isolated wrong sentence. It is a narrative attractor built into the pack's organization:

```text
finite probes
  → quotients
  → density carriers
  → projective/mixed geometry
  → Hopf/chirality
  → cuts/correlations
  → processes/history
  → telemetry
  → Pareto comparison
  → final admission
  → LevOS
```

That is a coherent, familiar information-theory and software-governance pipeline. It is also close to the kind of conventional reconstruction that the owner's model is meant to resist.

The current owner model is not merely a standard QIT pipeline with stricter receipts. Its distinctive pressures include:

- constraints before a furnished ontology;
- the full no-primitive discipline, not only a general statement about operational equivalence;
- MSS as weakest/minimal presumptions and leaps supporting evolving persistent structure;
- geometry and entropy ratcheted as one coupled construction, not geometry attached after an information carrier;
- a nested constraint manifold whose parts constrain one another through the whole;
- ring-checkerboard recursion plus nested shell/Hopf presentations as a proposed literal manifold organization, not merely a detached fixture;
- spinor/chirality as a global lift or foundational orientation candidate, not automatically a late layer;
- noncommutation and nonassociativity retained as distinct root pressures or explicitly resolved candidate roles;
- Axis 0 as the entropy-geometry gradient of the whole manifold, not merely a telemetry vector used by Pareto settlement;
- two independent 720-degree engine types, each with two coupled 360-degree loops, with optional type-1/type-2 co-location in an engine field;
- LevOS/ClaimGate intended to surround and constrain LLM execution, not only receive a capability after final scientific admission.

The pack does contain sentences that say many of these things. The problem is that its diagrams, schemas, prerequisites, work-package order, and active source manuals often encode something else. An LLM will preferentially follow the executable-looking structure rather than the caveat.

### Result summary

| Class | Count | Result |
|---|---:|---|
| Critical identity/root findings | 7 | active-default use should be quarantined pending owner correction |
| High collapse findings | 8 | amend in the next pack; several require owner decisions |
| Medium residual-control findings | 3 | do not treat as enforcement or settled architecture |
| Explicitly preserved owner structures | 7 | retain, with the qualifications below |

The v1 pack remains useful as:

- a recovery archive;
- a threat and hostile-test source;
- a repository/Gemini evidence index;
- a candidate governance architecture;
- a negative regression fixture for its own narrative collapse.

It should not yet be treated as the definitive boot ontology for implementing the Ratchet or manifold.

---

## 2. Authority basis used in this audit

### 2.1 Current owner constraints applied

This audit applies the following current/high-authority owner corrections from the active project continuity:

1. Codex-Ratchet is owner-designated v8, while old versions remain separate and may contribute only through explicit lineage and re-admission.
2. The wiki and LLM work are fuel, not canon.
3. LevOS is separate; the local LevOS checkout must not be changed.
4. ClaimGate is external and is intended to become a deterministic control system around LLM actions and LevOS use.
5. Julia is the reference lane; JAX is the workhorse; NumPy is not the main workhorse; PyTorch is support/training. Earlier component-level `no NumPy` instructions must not be silently erased.
6. The Ratchet is deterministic and cannot ratchet its own root constraints.
7. MSS means weakest/minimal presumptions and leaps that support evolving persistent structure; it is not an LLM score.
8. The manifold is nested and relational; inner and enclosing structures constrain one another.
9. Geometry and entropy are one coupled ratcheted structure at every admitted manifold depth.
10. Axis 0 is the entropy-geometry gradient of the whole manifold, not the last layer or a scalar.
11. Ring-checkerboard recursion and nested shell/Hopf presentations are part of the proposed literal manifold organization, not merely decoration.
12. Spinor/chiral orientation is global/foundational in the current proposal and must not be moved late merely to fit a conventional density-geometry ladder.
13. Deduction is `N_e → S_i → S_e → N_i`.
14. Induction is `N_e → N_i → S_e → S_i`.
15. Type 1/Left has outer Deduction and inner Induction.
16. Type 2/Right has outer Induction and inner Deduction.
17. Each type has an unreset outer-to-inner handoff.
18. There are 16 positions and four candidate bindings per position: 64 candidate cells, not 64 stages.
19. Engine-field nodes may be type 1, type 2, or both, and each node identity is a complete engine construction rather than a stage.
20. IGT preserves `WINwin`, `WINlose`, `LOSEwin`, and `LOSElose`; casing is not moral value.

### 2.2 Root-constraint set that must not remain implicit

The continuity context also contains the owner's named no-primitive/root discipline:

```text
NPI  no primitive identity
NPE  no primitive equality
NPC  no primitive causality
NPT  no primitive time
NPM  no primitive metric
NCO  no primitive Cartesian structure
NPF  no privileged frame
NPP  no primitive probability
FI   finitude
NCI  no completed infinities
NPD  no Platonic dualism
NOP  no observer privilege
NTL  no thermodynamic literalism
NFS  no FTL control
NSF  no singular future
NNF  no narrative-first explanation
```

Whether every code/name above remains the owner's exact current registry is itself an owner decision. The pack may not silently replace the registry with a shorter generic principle while claiming to preserve the root.

### 2.3 Important distinction

Owner authority establishes the intended project object and architecture. It does not establish that the model is mathematically consistent or physically true. This audit therefore says:

- `owner-aligned` or `owner-conflicting`;
- not `scientifically true` or `scientifically false`.

## 4. Detailed findings

### `AR01-AUTH-001` — The active owner root is incomplete and over-softened

**Severity:** Critical  
**Category:** owner authority / nominalist root  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `01_OWNER_MODEL/NOMINALIST_CONSTRAINT_MODEL.md:27-43` reduces “constraints precede axioms” to a general five-step method and a list of things it does not ban.
- `01_OWNER_MODEL/NOMINALIST_CONSTRAINT_MODEL.md:68-80` explicitly treats only primitive identity.
- The active owner files do not contain the named `NPI/NPE/NPC/NPT/NPM/NCO/NPF/NPP/...` registry.
- A partial unnamed no-primitive table appears only much later in `14_SOURCES/ORIENTATION_SOURCE_20260723.md:229-247`, a raw-source file that the load protocol says does not control active authority.
- `03_LLM_THREAT_MODEL/ANTI_PARADIGM_PATCHES_CORRECTED.md:832-848` summarizes the whole no-primitive discipline as “delayed commitment.”

**Owner lock**

The owner's model is explicitly built against primitive identity, equality, causality, time, metric/Cartesian structure, frame privilege, primitive probability, completed infinity, observer privilege, thermodynamic literalism, FTL-control inference, a singular future, and narrative-first explanation. Those are not interchangeable slogans.

**Collapse**

“Delayed commitment” is a useful operational gloss, but it can soften a root prohibition into a conventional boot sequence:

```text
do not assume X at line 1
→ install the familiar X later
→ call the project nominalist
```

Conversely, without a typed registry, another model may overreact and ban computational equality, clocks, probability distributions, metrics, or native continuum mathematics everywhere. The pack correctly warns against those bans, but it does not provide the per-constraint boundary needed to prevent both errors.

**Rival/charitable reading**

The pack may have intended to avoid turning philosophical constraints into impossible software prohibitions. That correction is valuable and should remain. It does not justify omitting the root registry or deciding that every no-primitive constraint means the same thing.

**Consequence**

Every later carrier, metric, causal test, probability model, clock, frame, thermodynamic analogy, and object identity can be admitted under a generic “licensed later” sentence without checking the specific owner constraint it risks violating.

**Required disposition**

`NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION` on the exact current root registry and status of each entry. The next pack should contain a compact, owner-reviewable table with:

- stable constraint ID;
- exact owner wording;
- prohibited root assumption;
- locally licensed operational uses;
- what cannot ever be inferred from those uses;
- falsifier or hostile test;
- supersession status.

Do not install the partial table from raw source as canon without owner review.

---

### `AR01-AUTH-002` — Generated manuals silently elevate themselves

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** authority inversion  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `12_AGENT_CONTRACTS/AGENT_OPERATING_CONTRACTS_AND_BUILD_ORDER_SOURCE.md:1-8` calls itself an “Operational control manual” that “turns Gemini's proposed agent prompts into an operational system.”
- The same file at `:126-171` labels a broad collection of reconstructed statements “Owner-locked architecture.”
- `04_DETERMINISTIC_CONTROL/EXECUTION_GOVERNANCE_AND_STACK_HARDENING.md:1-10` calls itself an “operating specification, implementation boundary, and acceptance contract” with “prescriptive continuation context.”
- That file's authority order gives “this execution manual and its two named companion manuals” priority over pinned repository evidence.
- These files live in active task directories, not only under `14_SOURCES/`.

**Owner lock**

LLM prose, earlier generated manuals, and detailed plans are proposal fuel unless a field is explicitly owner-locked or admitted through a separate process.

**Collapse**

The pack's short front door says the pack is informative. Its longest operational-looking documents then claim ownership of status vocabularies, two ClaimGate seals, work-package dependencies, ladders, and implementation gates. A model following the task-specific load route can reasonably treat those generated choices as approved architecture.

**Rival/charitable reading**

The files are useful source manuals and contain many strong controls. Their content need not be discarded. Their placement and standing are the error.

**Consequence**

Generated governance becomes a self-authorizing constitution. This is exactly the authority drift the pack says it is designed to stop.

**Required disposition**

`QUARANTINE_ACTIVE_DEFAULT`. Move exact source manuals to sourced-proposal/provenance space in the next release. Replace them in active paths with a shorter normalized contract where each prescriptive field is labeled:

- `OWNER_LOCKED`;
- `OWNER_APPROVED_CONTRACT`;
- `PACK_PROPOSAL`;
- `REPO_REPORTED`;
- `NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION`.

### `AR01-MANIFOLD-001` — Geometry and entropy are separated by the active diagram

**Severity:** Critical  
**Category:** manifold identity  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/CURRENT_ENTROPIC_GEOMETRIC_MANIFOLD.md:9-18` defines the active path `CTX → QUOT → DENS → PURE/MIX → HOPF → CHIR → CUT → CORR → PROC → HIST`.
- `:98-121` explicitly calls `PURE` and `MIX` the “projective geometric lift” and “mixed/purification geometric lift.”
- `:26-96` gives `CTX`, `QUOT`, and `DENS` their information/quotient/carrier roles before those geometric lifts.
- `09_WORK_ORDERS/DEPENDENCY_AWARE_BACKLOG.md:474-790` builds semantic carrier, N=3, Purgatory, Axis 0, and engine execution before the later “Geometry, topology, carrier” phase at `:909`.

**Owner lock**

Geometry and entropy are not independent ingredients that are joined after density carriers. The proposed manifold ratchets entropy/information and geometry together at every admitted depth. Each layer/depth requires both its entropy/information object and its geometry from the same distinctions and constraints.

**Collapse**

The pack reproduces a standard pattern:

```text
information state first
→ information geometry later
```

Calling the resulting whole “entropic-geometric” does not repair the separation.

**Rival/charitable reading**

The diagram is a plausible QIT-to-information-geometry branch and could be valuable as a serious rival. `PURE/MIX` being parallel is correctly preserved. The problem is its promotion to “Current Entropic–Geometric Manifold.”

**Consequence**

Implementers can finish `CTX/QUOT/DENS` as non-geometric structures, then attach Fubini–Study/Bures/Hopf objects as optional enrichments. That directly removes the owner's core claim that entropy and geometry are the same coupled ratcheting construction.

**Required disposition**

`QUARANTINE_ACTIVE_DEFAULT` and `KEEP_AS_RIVAL` under an ID such as `RIVAL_QIT_GEOMETRY_LIFT_CHAIN`. The owner-aligned active manifold contract must require, for every proposed depth:

- distinction/constraint carrier;
- entropy/information quantity;
- corresponding geometry or geometry-generating relation;
- downward restriction;
- plural upward extension;
- compatibility with the whole;
- serious alternative.

### `AR01-MANIFOLD-002` — “Diagram, not rung list” remains a mostly linear stack

**Severity:** Critical  
**Category:** nested manifold / narrative flattening  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/CURRENT_ENTROPIC_GEOMETRIC_MANIFOLD.md:7-22` presents a directed near-chain.
- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/IDENTITY_CHECKSUM.md:26-43` repeats the same arrow order as the checksum a model must reproduce.
- `CURRENT_ENTROPIC_GEOMETRIC_MANIFOLD.md:283-287` says the whole is relational, but the displayed diagram contains no reverse constraints, extension arrows, cross-depth maps, shell nesting, or concurrent whole-to-local constraint edges.
- `09_WORK_ORDERS/DEPENDENCY_AWARE_BACKLOG.md` serializes the scientific build into Phase C carrier/process work followed by Phase D geometry/topology.

**Owner lock**

The manifold is a nested constraint manifold:

```text
inner survives only if extendable into the whole nest
enclosing compresses inner
inner constrains enclosing
every active depth settles together
```

It is not only a feed-forward data pipeline.

**Collapse**

The word `WHOLE` is added after a unidirectional chain, but the implementation dependencies still instruct an LLM to build the chain one layer at a time. The caveat is weaker than the arrows.

**Rival/charitable reading**

A directed diagram can be a dependency display rather than a chronological ontology. The pack says this in prose. If that is the intent, it must encode both restriction and extension/compatibility relations rather than rely on the reader not to literalize the arrows.

**Consequence**

The resulting code can instantiate eleven data records and run a forward validator while claiming relational settlement. Local successes will define later layers instead of every branch being tested as a whole compatible nest.

**Required disposition**

`NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION` on the current manifold graph and exact layer/depth ordering. Until then:

- keep the CTX-to-HIST graph as one rival presentation;
- remove it from the mandatory identity checksum;
- represent restriction, extension, and cross-cut constraints explicitly in both directions;
- require at least two whole-diagram rivals.

### `AR01-MANIFOLD-003` — The ring-checkerboard is demoted from manifold proposal to detached fixture

**Severity:** Critical  
**Category:** loss of owner model  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

**Owner lock**

The current owner correction identifies ring-checkerboard recursion plus nested shells/Hopf tori as the literal proposed entropic-geometric constraint manifold. Flat, shell, and ring/Hopf views can be presentations/charts/carriers of that object without being globally identical manifolds.

**Collapse**

The pack correctly refuses to equate the checkerboard with the engine or Axis 0. It then overcorrects by removing it from the manifold's organizing structure.

**Rival/charitable reading**

Using a bounded ring-checkerboard as a test fixture is legitimate. “Can be a fixture” does not imply “is only a fixture.”

**Consequence**

An implementation can build a conventional density/cut/process manifold and later bolt on a ring visualization experiment, losing the owner's original geometric recursion.

`AMEND_NEXT_PACK`. Restore two separately typed roles:

Neither role identifies the ring with Axis 0 or with the scientific engines.

### `AR01-MSS-001` — MSS is replaced by generic Pareto optimization

**Severity:** Critical  
**Category:** Ratchet identity  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `01_OWNER_MODEL/NOMINALIST_CONSTRAINT_MODEL.md:229-253` defines MSS through a list of resource, repair, residual, optionality, and other comparison orders and says a non-dominated set is acceptable.
- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/RATCHET_MSS_SETTLEMENT_AND_PURGATORY.md:54-70` operationalizes MSS as several preorders followed by non-domination.
- `09_WORK_ORDERS/DEPENDENCY_AWARE_BACKLOG.md:667-724` fuses “Axis-0 and Pareto settlement.”
- `04_DETERMINISTIC_CONTROL/EXECUTION_GOVERNANCE_AND_STACK_HARDENING.md:912-927` makes Pareto-style settlement the corrected order.

**Owner lock**

MSS is weakest/minimal presumptions and leaps that lead to evolving persistent structure under nested constraints. It is not synonymous with:

- generic multi-objective optimization;
- lowest resource use;
- best predictive score;
- a metric frontier;
- or whatever coordinates happen to be available.

The known finite Ratchet work also has a more specific partition/distinguishability/coface mechanism that must be grounded from current code and receipts rather than replaced by a generic optimizer.

**Collapse**

The pack warns that Pareto is not Axis 0, but it still makes Pareto the practical Ratchet. “Presumption inclusion” appears as one objective beside resource, residual, retention, and repair cost. This permits trade-offs against the very weakest-presumption condition MSS is meant to enforce.

**Rival/charitable reading**

Partial orders and plural incomparable survivors are valuable. Pareto analysis can be an auxiliary view when the packet truly contains multiple non-compensatory relations. It does not define MSS by itself.

**Consequence**

An LLM can choose familiar measurable objectives, encode them as typed directions, and obtain an apparently deterministic “MSS frontier” that has no operational relationship to minimal presumptions or evolving persistence.

**Required disposition**

`NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION` on the exact current MSS comparator. Split:

1. root admissibility;
2. MSS/minimal-presumption relation;
3. persistence/evolvability sufficiency;
4. packet-specific performance/resource comparisons;
5. optional Pareto display.

Do not allow a candidate that is non-minimal in presumptions to compensate by being cheaper or scoring better unless the owner explicitly defines that relation.

- merely “telemetry”;
- the Pareto comparator;
- or a requirement to produce a ranking.

The owner also associates Axis 0 with homeostasis/exploration behavior, which the pack does not operationally preserve.

**Collapse**

The pack states the correct definition in prose, but its acceptance criteria make Axis 0 real only when it helps rank candidates. A genuine gradient/cofield that yields no dominated pair on one fixture would fail the pack's acceptance test.

**Rival/charitable reading**

Typed coordinates, seam residuals, and cross-map compatibility are strong implementation ideas. Pareto comparison may consume some Axis-0 outputs after they are independently defined.

**Consequence**

Implementers will optimize for a vector of familiar information-theory statistics rather than derive the manifold's entropy-geometry gradient. This can recreate “entropy soup” as a typed dictionary without illegal addition.

**Required disposition**

`QUARANTINE_ACTIVE_DEFAULT`. Split `WP-130` into:

1. Axis-0 semantic/mechanical definition and whole-manifold compatibility;
2. Axis-0 measurement/receipt implementation;
3. Ratchet consumption of licensed Axis-0 information;
4. optional packet-specific Pareto comparison.

Dominance must not be an Axis-0 existence criterion.

---

### `AR01-ENGINE-001` — The machine schema promotes a label into physical handedness

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** engine semantics  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `11_SCHEMAS/scientific_engine_schedule_v1.proposed.schema.json:45-67` requires `handedness` and fixes it to `LEFT` for Type 1.
- The same schema at `:73-95` fixes `handedness` to `RIGHT` for Type 2.
- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/IDENTITY_CHECKSUM.md:72` correctly says left/right requires an explicit chirality representation.
- `SCIENTIFIC_ENGINE_MECHANICS_AND_CONFORMANCE.md:143-145` also says a sign-flipped pair is only a surrogate.

**Owner lock**

Type 1/Left and Type 2/Right are protected orientation labels in the engine proposal. A Weyl/chiral realization requires an explicit representation and witness.

**Collapse**

A JSON instance can satisfy the `handedness` field by printing the required word. Schema validity can then be misreported as chiral conformance.

**Required disposition**

`AMEND_NEXT_PACK`. Separate:

```text
owner_orientation_label: TYPE_1_LEFT | TYPE_2_RIGHT
mathematical_handedness_status: UNREPRESENTED | PROPOSED | WITNESSED
representation_receipt: optional/required by claim
```

The schedule schema may freeze labels and order; it may not certify physical or algebraic chirality.

---

### `AR01-ENGINE-002` — “Coupled engines” is ambiguous about what is coupled

**Severity:** Medium  
**Category:** engine identity wording  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `00_START_HERE/README.md:20-22` refers to “two coupled 720-degree scientific information engines.”
- The authoritative engine files more carefully state two independent engine types and two coupled loops per type.

**Owner lock**

The two types are fully independent engine types. Within each type, the outer and inner 360-degree loops are coupled by a shared manifold and unreset handoff. Cross-type pairing/coupling is optional and belongs to the engine-field model.

**Consequence**

A model may implement one coupled left/right super-engine rather than two independently runnable engines.

**Required disposition**

`AMEND_NEXT_PACK` wording to:

> “two independent 720-degree engine types, each containing two coupled 360-degree loops.”

### `AR01-FIELD-001` — The engine-field formalization excludes a both-types-at-one-node case

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** engine field  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/IGT_FEP_AND_ENGINE_FIELD_RESEARCH_CONTRACT.md:100-114` defines `V = V_1 \sqcup V_2`, where every node in one partition hosts Type 1 and every node in the other hosts Type 2.
- It represents interaction only through an optional pairing relation between partitions.
- `SCIENTIFIC_ENGINE_MECHANICS_AND_CONFORMANCE.md:270-279` says each node is Type 1 or Type 2 and compares paired dyads.
- `DEPENDENCY_AWARE_BACKLOG.md:1013-1029` asks for a graph of Type-1/Type-2 nodes and paired field comparisons.

**Owner lock**

The owner explicitly allows a field node to contain:

- a complete Type-1 engine;
- a complete Type-2 engine;
- or both complete engine types.

A co-located both-types node is not necessarily equivalent to two nodes connected by a pairing edge.

**Collapse**

The disjoint union imports a convenient graph partition and deletes one of the owner's three node classes.

**Rival/charitable reading**

Separate paired nodes are a serious rival implementation and may be easier to test. They must not replace co-location by definition.

**Required disposition**

`AMEND_NEXT_PACK`. Use a node payload such as:

```text
engine_payload(v) ∈ {TYPE_1, TYPE_2, BOTH}
```

Retain separate-node pairing as a different field branch and build a discriminator for co-location versus dyadic coupling.

### `AR01-SIM-001` — Global NumPy supersession erases component-scoped prohibitions

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** version/engine policy  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `02_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE/GROUNDED_CONTINUITY_AND_EXECUTION_MAP.md:45-49` says the current Sim Engine policy “supersedes older ‘NumPy forbidden’ language.”
- `05_SIM_ENGINES/SIM_ENGINE_TRUST_MODEL.md:32-47` permits NumPy as satellite/exact-small support.

**Owner lock/conflict**

The active owner instruction permits NumPy as satellite support and explicitly forbids it as the main workhorse. Earlier/current component plans also contain strong `no NumPy` requirements for particular native arrows, spinor-network paths, or cross-framework independence tests.

**Collapse**

A global backend role and a per-arrow dependency prohibition are different fields. The pack uses the global role to supersede all older local prohibitions.

**Rival/charitable reading**

The pack correctly rejects the claim that NumPy is universally forbidden from every diagnostic or exact-small use. That does not decide every component contract.

**Consequence**

An agent can add NumPy to a core path and defend it as “satellite,” even when a specific arrow was meant to prove native Julia/JAX/PyTorch independence without NumPy.

**Required disposition**

`NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION` for each current load-bearing arrow. The next policy should say:

- global default: NumPy may be satellite/support;
- per-arrow override: `NUMPY_FORBIDDEN`, `NUMPY_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY`, or `NUMPY_ALLOWED_NONLOAD`;
- no global supersession without a field-level record.

### `AR01-VERSION-001` — “v1–v7 are historical” is broader than the owner's no-merge rule

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** version authority  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `03_LLM_THREAT_MODEL/LLM_BIAS_COVERT_CHEAT_AND_CONTROL_MODEL.md:42-44` says “CodexRatchet v8 is current. v1–v7 are provenance and test fuel.”
- `01_OWNER_MODEL/OWNER_LOCKS_AUTHORITY_AND_SUPERSESSION.md:31-34` more carefully says previous versions must not be merged merely because they resemble one another.
- `00_START_HERE/STATUS_AND_NONCLAIMS.md:55-61` notes that no `system_v8` front door was established and the repo advertises other versions.

**Owner lock**

The owner calls the current overall project/repository v8 and warns that systems mix prior versions. That does not prove every path carrying an older label is inactive. Mature objects may be explicitly inherited, repaired, or re-admitted.

**Collapse**

The pack converts “do not silently conflate” into “all earlier versions are historical fuel.” This can delete current inherited code just as surely as blind merging can corrupt v8.

**Required disposition**

`NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION` through a v8 authority/lineage manifest:

- active v8 object ID;
- exact source path/commit;
- inherited-from relation;
- semantic changes;
- active/superseded status by field;
- current receipt status.

Do not use folder/version number alone to include or exclude an object.

### `AR01-LEVOS-001` — The main topology places LevOS at the end instead of around execution

**Severity:** Critical  
**Category:** LevOS/ClaimGate role  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `02_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE/SYSTEM_BOUNDARIES_AND_TRUST_TOPOLOGY.md:23-35` draws `CG2 → external LevOS bridge → LevOS`.
- The same file at `:168-181` defines the allowed direction ending in “external host bridge.”
- `02_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE/CONTROL_FLOW_AND_TWO_CLAIMGATE_SEALS.md:138-152` calls the host transaction “a separate final phase.”
- `04_DETERMINISTIC_CONTROL/EXECUTION_GOVERNANCE_AND_STACK_HARDENING.md:1606` says “LevOS comes last in this sequence.”
- In tension with those statements, `CONTROL_FLOW_AND_TWO_CLAIMGATE_SEALS.md:44-55` lists a host/LevOS transaction among execution lanes, and `06_CLAIMGATE_LEVOS/EXTERNAL_BRIDGE_AND_FAIL_CLOSED_PROTOCOL.md:236-245` says the long-term goal is for host constraints to surround LLMs.

**Owner lock**

LevOS is intended to become the systems OS in which LLM activity runs. ClaimGate is meant to bridge and deterministically constrain actual LLM/tool execution, not merely deploy a scientific capability after it has already been proposed, executed, settled, and admitted elsewhere.

**Collapse**

The pack contains two incompatible topologies:

1. LevOS as final deployment/host-attestation target;
2. LevOS/ClaimGate as the execution envelope around agents and tools.

The main diagrams choose the first because it fits a conventional CI/CD release pipeline.

**Rival/charitable reading**

A final capability-exposure transaction is useful and should remain. Host integration cannot make bad science valid. That does not require LevOS to be absent from proposal/execution control.

**Consequence**

Most project work can bypass LevOS by design and only use it for a harmless final C7 demo, while the pack still claims alignment with the goal that LLMs run inside the deterministic system.

**Required disposition**

`NEEDS_OWNER_DECISION` on the exact initial and target topology. The next pack should represent two distinct flows:

```text
A. host-mediated execution/control:
LevOS public path ↔ external ClaimGate broker → bounded agent/tool/sim action

B. mature capability exposure:
scientific admission → capability package → external bridge → LevOS-hosted use
```

Neither flow may modify LevOS. One does not replace the other.

---

### `AR01-CLAIMGATE-001` — The two-seal pipeline is a strong proposal presented as required architecture

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** ClaimGate scope  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `02_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE/CONTROL_FLOW_AND_TWO_CLAIMGATE_SEALS.md:1-5` says two seals “are required.”
- `SYSTEM_BOUNDARIES_AND_TRUST_TOPOLOGY.md:12-16` installs `CG1` and `CG2` into the main topology.
- `12_AGENT_CONTRACTS/LLM_AGNOSTIC_AGENT_CONSTITUTION.md:117-157` creates separate intake and final-admission roles.
- `11_SCHEMAS/agent_capability_manifest_v1.proposed.schema.json:31-41` hardcodes those roles into the machine vocabulary.

**Owner lock**

ClaimGate is an external deterministic control/gating/tool system. It is distinct from the scientific Ratchet and must not self-certify. The owner has not, in the available high-authority material, locked the implementation to exactly two seals or to a conventional pre-execution/final-admission lifecycle.

**Collapse**

A useful threat-model pattern is promoted into system identity. It also narrows ClaimGate toward claim-envelope policy while the owner's intended ClaimGate includes tool control, object perception/admission, hooks, agent constraints, and potentially OS mediation.

**Rival/charitable reading**

Separating route eligibility from final evidence admission is a strong security design. It should remain a leading architecture candidate.

**Required disposition**

`KEEP_AS_RIVAL`. Relabel as `PROPOSED_TWO_SEAL_CLAIMGATE_ARCHITECTURE` until owner-approved and tested against:

- a single state-machine implementation with separated authorities;
- multiple independent gates;
- host-broker/action-gate topology;
- existing LevOS capabilities that may already solve parts of the problem.

---

### `AR01-SCHEMA-001` — The proposed claim envelope makes LevOS universal

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** machine-contract contradiction  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- `11_SCHEMAS/claim_envelope_v1.proposed.schema.json:7-18` requires `host` for every claim.
- `:57-99` requires `levos_commit`, `session_id`, at least one `event_id`, bridge policy digest, and pre/post tree digests.
- Elsewhere the pack says host evidence is required “where applicable” and C7 is tuple-scoped, not universal.

**Owner lock**

The workstreams must remain independently testable. A Julia/JAX exact-small arrow, mathematical audit, or pre-host manifold fixture can be valid without claiming LevOS execution.

**Collapse**

The prose preserves independent maturity, but the machine schema forces every claim through LevOS. Implementers will either:

- fabricate placeholder host data;
- misuse one old host receipt;
- or block all non-C7 science.

**Required disposition**

`QUARANTINE_ACTIVE_DEFAULT`. Use a discriminated union:

```text
host_scope = NOT_APPLICABLE | REQUIRED | ATTESTED | PENDING | BLOCKED
```

Only `ATTESTED` includes nonempty LevOS evidence. `NOT_APPLICABLE` must not be interpreted as C7.

---

### `AR01-PROMPT-001` — The boot prompt remains self-attested behavior

**Severity:** Medium  
**Category:** prompt/control boundary  
**Status:** Open residual risk  

**Pack evidence**

- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/IDENTITY_CHECKSUM.md:3` says an agent “must reproduce this checksum before changing” the system.
- `00_START_HERE/MODEL_BOOT_PROMPT.md:42-60` asks the model to self-report files read, checksum reproduction, readiness, and requested writes.
- `12_AGENT_CONTRACTS/LLM_AGNOSTIC_AGENT_CONSTITUTION.md:7-18` supplies constitutional prohibitions.
- The same constitution correctly states at `:12` and `:32` that no prompt is a security boundary and that the frame is only a reporting contract.

**Owner lock**

Prompts and role text are behavioral aids. Deterministic capability restrictions, protected roots, process identities, and separate verifiers must enforce real transitions.

**Collapse**

The pack knows this limitation, but a repository-connected agent can still set:

```yaml
owner_checksum_reproduced: true
ready_for_bounded_work: true
```

without reading or preserving anything. No pack component independently verifies the boot receipt or binds it to actual access logs/capabilities.

**Required disposition**

`AMEND_NEXT_PACK`. Explicitly label boot output `UNTRUSTED_MODEL_ORIENTATION_RECEIPT`. It must never authorize writes or promotion. Any real gate must independently supply:

- immutable pack digest;
- externally assigned task/write scope;
- sandbox or filesystem enforcement;
- policy digest outside agent write access;
- post-action path audit.

---

### `AR01-SALIENCE-001` — Pack size and duplicate active manuals create an LLM attention attack

**Severity:** High  
**Category:** context/salience collapse  
**Status:** Open  

**Pack evidence**

- Round zero already records more than 120,000 Markdown words.
- Active directories contain:
  - a 2,065-line Gemini agent/build-order source manual;
  - a 1,704-line execution manual;
  - a 2,494-line GPU program;
  - a 1,823-line backlog;
  - a 1,136-line audit protocol.
- Several restate authority, status ladders, ClaimGate topology, Axis 0, manifold order, and engine semantics with small differences.
- `00_START_HERE/LOAD_ORDER_AND_TASK_PROTOCOL.md:32-43` routes a model into these task directories after the compact checksum.

**Owner lock**

A long fluent synthesis does not outrank a short owner correction. Versions and rival readings must not be averaged.

**Collapse**

The pack's defense against context drift is itself a context-overload artifact. Models tend to:

- compress repeated text into a single conventional story;
- privilege implementation-shaped work orders over philosophical locks;
- treat repeated generated choices as consensus;
- forget a short contradiction from the owner packet.

**Required disposition**

`AMEND_NEXT_PACK`. Create a small owner-reviewable boot packet containing only:

- root constraints;
- active system boundaries;
- engine identity;
- current conflicts;
- prohibited inferences;
- exact evidence pointers.

Keep the massive archive, but load detailed manuals only by stable task ID. No generated source manual should be in the active authority path.

## 5. Structures that the pack does preserve

These are real alignments and should not be lost while correcting the findings.

### `AR01-ALIGN-ENGINE-001` — Engine schedule and count

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`

The active pack consistently preserves:

```text
Deduction: N_e → S_i → S_e → N_i
Induction: N_e → N_i → S_e → S_i

Type 1 / Left: outer Deduction, inner Induction
Type 2 / Right: outer Induction, inner Deduction

unreset outer-to-inner state and record handoff
16 positions
4 candidate bindings per position
64 candidate cells, not 64 stages
4^16 unconstrained complete assignments before earned reduction
```

Relevant paths:

- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/IDENTITY_CHECKSUM.md:45-73`
- `07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/SCIENTIFIC_ENGINE_MECHANICS_AND_CONFORMANCE.md:3-51`
- `11_SCHEMAS/scientific_engine_schedule_v1.proposed.schema.json`

Qualification: schedule conformance does not establish handedness, stage meanings, thermodynamic strokes, or physical realization.

### `AR01-ALIGN-IGT-001` — IGT casing

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`

`07_RATCHET_MANIFOLD_ENGINES/IGT_FEP_AND_ENGINE_FIELD_RESEARCH_CONTRACT.md:3-23` preserves:

- `WINwin`;
- `WINlose`;
- `LOSEwin`;
- `LOSElose`;
- uppercase outer/lowercase inner;
- no moral good/bad identification;
- rival MAX/min, impedance, known/unknown, and FEP readings.

Qualification: the file calls them “states.” Until an engine/manifold mapping is earned, “casing patterns” or “casing combinations” is safer than an ontological state claim.

### `AR01-ALIGN-AXIS0-001` — Anti-scalar language

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT` for the prohibition only

The pack repeatedly and correctly rejects:

- Axis 0 as the final rung;
- Axis 0 as one scalar;
- summing unlike entropies;
- treating a generic Pareto dictionary as Axis 0.

The implementation ordering and dominance dependency remain defective under `AR01-AXIS0-001`.

### `AR01-ALIGN-BOUNDARY-001` — LevOS non-mutation

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`

The pack strongly preserves:

- separate LevOS ownership;
- external bridge;
- read-only checkout;
- no direct-shell fallback when claiming LevOS;
- pre/post tree evidence;
- tuple-scoped host claims.

The host's position in the overall topology remains unresolved under `AR01-LEVOS-001`.

### `AR01-ALIGN-PROMPT-001` — Prompt caveat

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`

The pack explicitly says prompts, councils, constitutions, and model consensus are not security boundaries. The remaining problem is that no mechanical implementation accompanies the behavioral boot flow.

### `AR01-ALIGN-EVIDENCE-001` — Determinism is not truth

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`

The pack consistently distinguishes:

- native execution from semantic correctness;
- hashes from authorization;
- cross-runtime agreement from independent semantics;
- finite enumeration from universal proof;
- GPU search from theorem lift;
- ClaimGate admission from unrestricted truth.

### `AR01-ALIGN-N3-001` — Three contexts versus three qubits

**Disposition:** `ACCEPT_ALIGNMENT`

The active work orders explicitly distinguish `N=3` graph/context renesting from a three-qubit density carrier. This is an important anti-number-collapse control.

## 6. Owner decisions required before a corrected boot pack

These questions cannot be answered safely by another LLM synthesis.

### `AR01-OD-001` — Exact root-constraint registry

Which named constraints are currently owner-locked, and what is the exact meaning/status of:

- NPI/NPE/NPC/NPT/NPM/NCO/NPF/NPP;
- FI/NCI/NPD/NOP/NTL/NFS/NSF/NNF;
- F01/N01;
- nonassociativity?

For each, is it:

- a root prohibition;
- a root pressure;
- a campaign axiom;
- an earned-structure rule;
- or a historical label?

### `AR01-OD-002` — Manifold active proposal

Is the active owner proposal:

1. ring-checkerboard recursion plus nested shells/Hopf/spinor organization as the literal manifold;
2. the CTX→QUOT→DENS→... diagram;
3. a typed mapping between those two;
4. or unresolved rival branches?

No hybrid should be generated without explicit maps.

### `AR01-OD-003` — Foundational position of spinor/chirality/nonassociativity

What must be globally present before any engine/manifold layer exists, and what remains a representation tournament?

### `AR01-OD-004` — MSS

What is the exact current executable MSS relation?

- Is there a root/meta-MSS before operational equivalence?
- Is the current code's partition/coface frontier authoritative test fuel?
- Must settlement return one best branch, a plural minimal frontier, or both at different phases?
- Which comparisons are hard/minimality relations and which are optional Pareto metrics?

### `AR01-OD-005` — Axis 0

What exact object makes Axis 0 the entropy-geometry gradient rather than a telemetry registry?

- carrier;
- local and transverse maps;
- homeostasis/exploration semantics;
- relation to ring-checkerboard gradient;
- relation to Ratchet comparison;
- whether it is logically prior to, simultaneous with, or measured after settlement.

### `AR01-OD-006` — NumPy scopes

Which arrows/packages are:

- NumPy forbidden;
- NumPy diagnostic only;
- NumPy allowed as non-load-bearing satellite?

### `AR01-OD-007` — v8 authority manifest

Which current paths/objects constitute v8, and which earlier-version objects are explicitly inherited rather than merely historical?

### `AR01-OD-008` — ClaimGate/LevOS topology

Does the first target topology require:

- LevOS/ClaimGate wrapping selected LLM and tool execution before scientific admission;
- final exposure of admitted capabilities through LevOS;
- or both as distinct transaction classes?

Is the two-seal ClaimGate design accepted, merely leading, or one rival?

---

## 7. Required amendment order

Do not repair the v1 pack in place. For a v2 candidate:

1. Obtain owner decisions `AR01-OD-001` through `AR01-OD-008`.
2. Produce a small signed/hashed owner packet; do not begin from the 120,000-word archive.
3. Demote the current CTX-to-HIST diagram to a rival unless explicitly owner-selected.
4. restore the ring/shell/Hopf/spinor manifold proposal as its own branch.
5. define geometry and entropy together at every candidate depth.
6. split root/meta-MSS, campaign MSS, performance comparisons, and Pareto display.
7. split Axis-0 mechanics from Axis-0 measurement and from Ratchet selection.
8. repair engine handedness schema and engine-field node types.
9. scope NumPy policy per arrow.
10. build the v8 lineage manifest.
11. represent host-mediated execution and post-admission LevOS exposure as separate flows.
12. mark the two-seal ClaimGate design as proposed unless owner-approved.
13. repair schemas before any validator treats them as active.
14. move generated source manuals out of the active authority path.
15. run a new cold-model hostile test specifically designed to see whether it reconstructs the conventional QIT/Pareto pipeline despite the owner packet.

---

## 8. Final claim

This audit establishes only the following bounded statement:

> The immutable v1 recovery pack contains multiple explicit owner-aligned safeguards, but its active diagrams, work-order dependencies, schemas, and generated operating manuals still encode a conventional linear QIT/Pareto/admission narrative that conflicts with or prematurely resolves important parts of the owner's nominalist constraint model.

It does not establish:

- the correct final manifold;
- the correct mathematics of Axis 0;
- the exact MSS comparator;
- the physical validity of spinor/chiral interpretations;
- the current local implementation state;
- or the scientific truth of the project.

## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Master finding ledger

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Master Finding Ledger

**Audit ID:** `AUDIT-RATPACK-20260723-R01`  
**Purpose:** reconcile independent audit lanes without averaging conflicts  
**Baseline mutation:** none  
**Fresh scientific execution:** none

## How to read this ledger

Several independent audits found overlapping defects using different evidence and terminology. This ledger groups those findings by object, but it does not merge their meanings or silently choose a repair.

Disposition vocabulary:

| Disposition | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `BLOCK_ACTIVE_DEFAULT` | The object may remain provenance or a fixture but must not define the active system. |
| `OWNER_DECISION_REQUIRED` | Competing interpretations remain open until the owner binds one or preserves plurality. |
| `NEEDS_MACHINE_EVIDENCE` | Static inspection cannot establish the claimed execution fact. |
| `KEEP_AS_NEGATIVE_REGRESSION` | Preserve the failed/substituted object as an attack fixture. |
| `PRESERVE` | An aligned protection that a later revision must retain. |
| `AMEND_NEXT_PACK` | Correct the derived pack while keeping immutable v1 unchanged. |

## Cross-cutting narrative-collapse patterns

### 1. Stable-name object substitution

The name remains while the mathematical object changes:

- “64” changes from rival bindings into runtime substages.
- “independent engines” changes from independent implementations into native wrappers around shared results.
- “MSS” changes from a root admission relation into non-dominated optimization.
- “Axis 0” changes from a transverse whole-manifold gradient into a late telemetry record.
- “LevOS consumed” changes from causal host execution into ingestion of a prior evidence envelope.
- “P versus NP” changes from an all-input complexity statement into a bounded density-matrix task.

Stable labels must therefore never count as lineage evidence.

### 2. Familiar-math attraction

LLMs prefer a stack that can be narrated with known objects:

```text
finite observations
→ quotient
→ Hilbert space
→ density operator
→ information geometry
→ Hopf/chiral feature
→ open-system process
→ Pareto selection
```

The stack is mathematically respectable, which makes the collapse harder to notice. Respectability does not establish that these are the weakest structures, that they emerge from the owner's root constraints, or that entropy and geometry co-generate one another.

### 3. Closed authorship disguised as independent evidence

An LLM can author the mechanism, expected output, control, validator, policy, and receipt. Different languages or libraries then replay the same authored answer. Hashes protect that loop from mutation but do not make it independent.

Required separation must be causal:

- different decisive implementations;
- independently specified fixtures or externally known results;
- validators unable to import the producer's result constructor;
- controls whose expected outcomes are not authored alongside the candidate;
- external or owner-bound policy roots;
- asymmetric mutations proving that each lane computes rather than decorates.

### 4. Salience as authority

Longer and more duplicated generated manuals can crowd out short owner-native mechanisms. Cold-start systems are especially vulnerable: they infer authority from organization, recency language, schema formality, and repeated cross-links.

The correction is not merely another large manual. The active owner checksum must be smaller, content-addressed, explicitly authoritative, and mechanically distinguished from fuel.

### 5. Passing the wrapper instead of the claim

A process can genuinely:

- import JAX;
- allocate a tensor;
- invoke Z3;
- compute a hash;
- parse a schema;
- start a LevOS command;
- or run on a GPU;

while the decisive observable was precomputed, hardcoded, inherited from a shared helper, or unrelated to the native scientific problem. Every acceptance gate needs a mechanism-specific causal witness.

## Protections to preserve

The audit is not a blanket rejection. A v2 candidate should retain the following v1 protections:

1. Exact owner engine application orders and the distinction between 16 positions and 64 candidate cells.
2. Unreset outer-to-inner state and record continuity.
3. Two independent engine types, each containing induction and deduction.
4. IGT casing without moralizing WIN/LOSE.
5. Finite dimension, finite state set, finite encoding, and finite physical universe remain distinct.
6. Equality is typed and operational; implementation equality is not silently promoted to ontology.
7. Noncommutation and nonassociativity remain distinct properties with distinct witnesses.
8. Heterogeneous entropy quantities are not summed into one scalar soup.
9. Serious negatives remain stronger than toys or strawmen.
10. LLMs and councils propose; they cannot settle MSS or self-promote.
11. Deterministic, reproducible, schema-valid, and hashed do not mean scientifically true.
12. GPU and annealing are bounded search/comparison tools, not proof oracles.
13. Native major-problem statements are not replaced by finite namesakes.
14. LevOS remains separate, protected from writes, and mandatory only where the claim contract requires its host path.
15. The full Gemini thread and historical repository remain provenance/fuel rather than execution evidence.

## Required correction order

This order is a dependency statement, not a decision on the disputed content:

1. Bind an owner-authority router and concrete v8 content manifest.
2. Bind or explicitly pluralize the no-primitive root registry.
3. Decide the generative relation between root constraints, MSS, probes, equivalence, and candidate carriers.
4. Specify rival manifold topologies without preinstalling a linear QIT ladder.
5. Decide the roles of ring-checkerboard, spinor/chirality/orientation, Axis 0, and Axes 4/5/6.
6. Freeze the owner engine schedule, 16×4 semantics, unreset handoff, and field configurations as regression tests.
7. Redesign claim schemas around typed applicability, independent policy authority, strict parsing, chronology, content binding, and causal witnesses.
8. Establish genuine Julia/JAX independence before describing a multi-engine seal.
9. Establish the external ClaimGate/LevOS trust boundary and live fail-closed evidence path without touching the LevOS checkout.
10. Only then implement Ratchet settlement, manifold experiments, engine tournaments, GPU campaigns, and major-problem tracks.

## Claim ceiling

This ledger establishes structural conflicts and verified code/schema weaknesses. It does not establish that the owner model is scientifically correct, that any proposed repair is uniquely right, that local or cloud execution currently works, or that a native mathematical/physical problem has advanced.

## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Mathematical and architectural collapse audit

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Mathematical and Architectural Collapse Audit

**Audit ID:** `MAR1-MATH-ARCH-20260724`  
**Audit target:** immutable recovery pack `RATCHET_LLM_AGNOSTIC_RECOVERY_AND_CONTROL_PACK_20260723_v1`  
**Audit date:** 2026-07-24  
**Scope:** nominalist root, nested entropy–geometry manifold, Ratchet/MSS, Purgatory and negative evidence, scientific/QIT engines, ring-checkerboard, Hopf/spinor/chirality, nonassociativity, IGT/FEP, Engine Field, and GPU/major-problem framing  
**Mutation policy:** the v1 pack was not edited  
**GitHub policy:** read-only connector inspection; no branch, file, issue, pull request, or repository was changed  
**Execution status:** document and source audit only; no scientific simulation was rerun  

---

## 1. Executive verdict

The v1 pack is a strong epistemic recovery artifact, but it is **not yet safe as the sole mathematical boot object** for an implementation agent.

It successfully blocks many obvious forms of model drift:

- finite dimension is not confused with a finite state set;
- equality kinds are typed;
- the two engine schedules are preserved correctly;
- `16 positions × 4 bindings` is not called 64 sequential stages;
- heterogeneous entropies are not licensed as one scalar soup;
- native open problems are not replaced by finite simulations;
- sign-flipped Hamiltonians are not accepted as chirality;
- noncommutation and nonassociativity are kept distinct;
- thermodynamic and FEP mappings are usually labeled candidates;
- GPU and annealing outputs are not treated as proof.

Those are real strengths.

However, the pack’s **front-door architecture** still performs several subtler collapses:

1. It labels the manifold a diagram while presenting a nearly linear standard-QIT dependency spine.
2. It installs complex density operators early enough that they are likely to become the de facto ontology before the carrier tournament runs.
3. It places Hopf/spinor/chiral structure downstream even though the owner’s more recent correction treats the spinor as an early or global lift and the ring-checkerboard as much closer to the literal proposed manifold.
4. It separates “geometry” into later `PURE/MIX/HOPF` lifts instead of requiring an entropy–geometry pair at every admitted depth.
5. It preserves the words “Axis 0 cofield” but reduces its first executable form to a telemetry tuple plus Pareto ordering.
6. It weakens MSS from “minimal presumption among sufficient persistent structures” into generic multi-objective non-domination.
7. It treats unreset concatenation of two four-stage loops as enough to protect the “720-degree” identity even though no spinorial \(4\pi\) path has yet been constructed.
8. It omits the current Axis-4/5/6 corrections from the actual cold-start checksum.
9. It lets conventional CPTP, thermodynamic, FEP, quantum-Darwinist, and graph language become default implementations before those structures have been earned from the nominalist root.
10. Its major-problem program is mathematically cautious but mostly a high-quality conventional computational-mathematics program; it does not yet show that the Ratchet/manifold supplies a distinctive method.

The pack therefore has two simultaneous statuses:

| Dimension | Verdict |
|---|---|
| Epistemic and anti-overclaim discipline | **Strong; preserve** |
| Exact current engine schedule | **Strong; preserve as owner architecture** |
| Current manifold topology/order | **Unresolved and partly collapsed** |
| Entropy–geometry unification | **Named correctly, not yet structurally realized** |
| Spinor/ring-checkerboard placement | **Conflicts with the more recent owner correction** |
| MSS formalization | **A better formula exists inside the pack’s source appendix, but the boot-facing core weakens it** |
| Axis 0 | **Owner meaning partly preserved; executable mathematics still open** |
| Native open-problem framing | **Strong claim ceilings; model-specific bridge absent** |
| GitHub alignment | **Useful negative evidence; current repository material must not be imported as authority** |

The correct response is not to discard v1. It is to preserve its control discipline while replacing its mathematical front door with an owner-resolved, carrier-neutral, non-linear architecture checksum.

## 2. Audit method and evidence hierarchy

### 2.1 Evidence classes

| Code | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `OWNER-ACTIVE` | Explicit current owner correction available to this audit; outranks pack and repository semantics |
| `PACK-CORE` | File loaded by the v1 cold-start protocol |
| `PACK-SOURCE` | Source appendix in v1; useful provenance, but not automatically owner authority |
| `PACK-CONFLICT` | Two v1 files encode materially different versions |
| `GITHUB-CURRENT-READ` | Read-only retrieval from the current named branch/path during this audit |
| `GITHUB-PINNED` | Read-only retrieval at a pinned commit |
| `NOT-RERUN` | Source or receipt inspected without fresh execution |

### 2.2 Finding classes

| Class | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `STRENGTH` | A protection that should be retained |
| `COLLAPSE` | A stronger or stranger project object has been replaced by a familiar narrative or standard object |
| `CONFLICT` | Higher-authority owner meaning, pack core, pack source, or GitHub disagree |
| `GAP` | An undefined seam permits a later model to choose the familiar answer silently |
| `MATH-DEFECT` | Formula, typing, or theorem-status error independent of interpretation |

### 2.3 Severity

- `BLOCKER`: unsafe to use as an implementation authority before correction.
- `CRITICAL`: likely to change the project’s object while preserving its nouns.
- `MAJOR`: materially biases implementation or scientific inference.
- `MODERATE`: bounded ambiguity with a plausible containment path.
- `MINOR`: local repair or wording issue.

## 3. Strengths that must not be lost during correction

These are listed first because a “repair” that deletes them would be another collapse.

### MAR1-STR-001 — Finite dimension, finite set, finite encoding, and finite physics are separated

**Class:** `STRENGTH`  
**Strength:** high

`01_OWNER_MODEL/NOMINALIST_CONSTRAINT_MODEL.md:58-66` explicitly distinguishes:

> “a finite-dimensional vector space over an infinite field”;  
> “a finite set of bit strings”;  
> “a finite machine representation”;  
> “a bounded experimental domain”;  
> “a physically finite system.”

It correctly notes that \(n\) qubits have dimension \(2^n\) while normalized complex amplitudes are not a finite set unless coefficients are bounded or discretized.

**Preserve:** this distinction must remain in every future manifold and GPU contract.
