## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Owner decisions required

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Owner Decisions Required Before Any v2 Semantic Rewrite

**Purpose:** preserve unresolved forks without letting an LLM select the most familiar story.  
**Rule:** absence of an owner decision leaves the item `OPEN`; it does not authorize the auditor, repository majority, newest file, or most executable branch to decide.

## How to answer

For each decision, the owner may:

- select one branch;
- keep multiple branches for a tournament;
- correct all listed branches;
- or defer.

Free-form corrections override the option labels. A selection changes semantic authority only after it is captured in a versioned owner checksum.

---

## `OD-01` — What is the strict root object, if any?

### Conflict

The compact pack begins with bounded addresses and probe-relative distinctions. A strict nominalist reading says even pre-individuated addresses, equality of outputs, probes-as-functions, and a finite set `X` are installed structures.

### Branches

- **A — Installed executable floor:** finite addresses and probe functions are allowed as a declared implementation substrate, never as derived ontology.
- **B — Strict nominalist floor:** no pre-individuated `X`; the first task is to formalize admissible distinction without primitive identity/equality.
- **C — Two-lane:** A for bounded computation, B as the foundational research obligation; no inference from A to B without a bridge.

### Safe provisional state

`OPEN`; use C operationally without claiming that A derives B.

## `OD-02` — Which root constraints belong in the owner checksum?

### Conflict

The active owner context names a broad no-primitive family. A fresh repo source lists C01–C19, but that source itself warns it may not reproduce the exact earlier wording and therefore cannot become canon merely by being detailed.

### Required owner action

Confirm, rename, remove, or amend:

- `NPI` no primitive identity;
- `NPE` no primitive equality;
- `NPC` no primitive causality;
- `NPT` no primitive time;
- `NPM` no primitive metric;
- `NCO` no Cartesian/origin;
- `NPF` no privileged frame;
- `NPP` no primitive probability;
- finitism / no completed infinities;
- no Platonic dualism;
- no observer privilege;
- no thermodynamic literalism;
- no controllable FTL signaling;
- no singular privileged future;
- no narrative-first inference;
- no primitive global optimization/evaluator;
- no free explanation.

### Safe provisional state

Treat every item as `OWNER-SOURCE CANDIDATE`; do not silently omit it and do not call the repo expansion canonical.

## `OD-03` — Does MSS constrain probes/equivalence, or begin after them?

### Conflict

One branch computes operational equivalence first and applies MSS to surviving carriers. The owner context also states that MSS is prior to operational equivalence and that the oracle precedes the Turing-machine side.

### Branches

- **A:** `probes → ~ → quotient → MSS`;
- **B:** `MSS admissibility → allowed probes/distinctions → ~`;
- **C:** mutually constraining fixed-point/co-ratchet, with no one-way first step;
- **D:** another owner-defined order.

### Safe provisional state

`OPEN`; prohibit any “root direction” from being called forced.

## `OD-04` — What exactly is MSS?

### Conflict

The pack turns MSS into packet-relative partial orders and a non-dominated frontier. The source foundation describes MSS as the weakest minimal-presumption, still-killable/compositional survivor meta-gate and explicitly distinguishes it from ordinary simplicity.

### Owner decision needed

Confirm whether:

- Pareto settlement is only a downstream reporting mechanism;
- MSS is an admission rule over presumed structures/closures;
- “survival,” “persistence,” and “evolvability” require a supplied update/time structure;
- MSS remains plural when incomparable;
- and which root comparator the Ratchet is forbidden to ratchet.

### Safe provisional state

Do not use `MSS` as a synonym for Pareto non-domination.

## `OD-05` — Is the ring-checkerboard native support or a late presentation?

### Conflict

The compact manifold places the ring/Hopf construction after density, pure, and mixed carriers. The owner describes ring-checkerboard/nested shells and its entropy gradient as central proposed manifold machinery.

### Branches

- **A — Native support:** ring-checkerboard/nested shells are the proposed literal finite manifold carrier from which later structures are tested.
- **B — Presentation:** it is a chart/visualization over a separately earned carrier.
- **C — Rival branches:** both remain live and must be compared for hidden presumptions and unique predictions.

### Safe provisional state

`OPEN`; preserve C and do not call either global identity established.

## `OD-06` — Where do spinors, chirality, and global orientation enter?

### Conflict

Repo branches use spinors as early closure witnesses, engine chirality, 720-degree memory carriers, and late graded observables. The compact pack chooses a late `HOPF → CHIR` branch.

### Owner decision needed

Indicate whether each role is:

- root/global;
- early lift;
- later earned carrier;
- engine-only structure;
- diagnostic representation;
- or a separate rival.

### Safe provisional state

Do not collapse these roles into one “spinor layer.”

## `OD-07` — What is owner-locked about Axis 0?

### Conflict

The owner lock appears to be the whole-manifold entropy–geometry gradient/cofield with homeostatic/exploratory significance. The compact pack promotes one typed set/union formula to “Current definition.” Older repo branches define Axis 0 as XOR, intuition/sensing, parity, a late loop property, or local response.

### Required separation

Confirm the authority of:

1. whole-manifold scope;
2. geometry–entropy gradient role;
3. homeostasis/exploration role;
4. ring-checkerboard gradient;
5. specific cofield carrier/formula;
6. local historical Axis-0 observables;
7. relationship to axes 1–6.

### Safe provisional state

Items 1–3 are owner-intent locks; items 4–7 remain named candidates unless the owner says otherwise.

## `OD-08` — What is the current manifold’s actual generative topology?

### Conflict

The pack offers `CTX → QUOT → DENS → PURE/MIX → HOPF → CHIR → CUT → CORR → PROC → HIST`. The owner describes nested, simultaneous layers in which geometry and entropy run together and each layer constrains every rising layer.

### Owner decision needed

Provide or approve:

- a non-linear graph;
- arrows that constrain downward/backward;
- which carriers are rival rather than sequential;
- where renesting occurs;
- where engines act;
- how entropy and geometry are paired at each node;
- and which elements are owner locks versus work hypotheses.

### Safe provisional state

Treat the compact chain as `MANIFOLD_CANDIDATE_A`, not the checksum.

## `OD-09` — What concrete content is Codex-Ratchet v8?

### Conflict

## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Deep manifold and engine audit report

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Deep audit: constrained deformation manifold, residual degrees of freedom, and engines

**Date:** 2026-07-26  
**Status:** bounded candidate audit  
**Promotion:** `promotion_allowed: false`

## Bottom line

The proposal is not empty: it contains a coherent finite research program.
Several components reduce to standard, executable mathematics:

1. finite distinguishability classes and Hartley capacity;
2. ordered maps whose composition can be noncommutative;
3. residual degrees of freedom after constraints;
4. directed cyclic dynamics on a phase-extended state space;
5. relative holonomy on a Hopf chart;
6. finite transition-system simulation;
7. packet-relative comparison by the existing ConstraintBox Ratchet.

The central emergence claim is not established. In particular:

- the tightest constraint set need not contain any residual degree of freedom;
- a scalar gradient cannot sustain a nonconstant periodic orbit;
- adding a skew part to a strictly dissipative gradient produces a spiral, not
  a sustained cycle;
- an undirected cycle or nonzero first Betti number does not establish an
  admissible directed engine traversal;
- the tested four-stroke quantum process has a periodic sequence only after an
  explicit phase/controller variable is supplied;
- the finite checkerboard support has not been shown to deform into the Hopf
  carrier. Those two ends were tested separately, not derived from one another;
- no tested equation identifies entropy with gravity.

The strongest honest formulation is therefore:

> A finite distinguishability support can be subjected to an ordered library
> of typed constrained deformations. Some surviving deformation towers may
> retain directed cyclic degrees of freedom. Those cycles can implement finite
> channels and finite transition systems. Whether the proposed engine family
> is selected by the Ratchet, rather than inserted into it, is an open
> experiment.

## 1. Exact finite base

Let

\[
A_n=\{0,1\}^n,\qquad X_{n,d}=A_n^d.
\]

`n` is local representation capacity and `d` is nesting depth. Then

\[
|X_{n,d}|=2^{nd},\qquad H_0(X_{n,d})=\log_2|X_{n,d}|=nd.
\]

The projection to the first \(k\) levels,

\[
\pi_k:X_{n,d}\to A_n^k,
\]

has constant extension fibres

\[
|\pi_k^{-1}(y)|=2^{n(d-k)}.
\]

This is a precise finite model of the flat nested-checkerboard analogy. In the
executed fixture \(n=2,d=3,k=1\):

| Observable | Result |
|---|---:|
| Complete addresses | 64 |
| Hartley capacity | 6 bits |
| Prefix classes | 4 |
| Completions per prefix | 16 |

Python and Node reproduced all four values independently.

This establishes a computational frame, not a theorem that abstract numbers
cannot be defined without a finite frame. The defensible claim is narrower:
every executed numerical result has a finite representation contract, and
comparisons across contracts require an explicit encoding or embedding.

Two distinct injections \(e_1,e_2:X_2\hookrightarrow X_3\) were constructed in
the audit, so finite frame growth is not unique:

\[
e_1(i)=i,\qquad e_2(i)=2i,\qquad i\in\{0,1,2,3\}.
\]

## 2. Formal Ratchet over deformation towers

Start with a finite observational object

\[
\mathcal M_0=(X_0,\Pi_0,D_0),
\]

where \(\Pi_0\) is a frozen probe family and \(D_0\subseteq X_0^2\) is the set
of demanded distinctions. Operational equivalence is

\[
x\sim_{\Pi_r} y
\iff
\forall p\in\Pi_r,\ p(x)=p(y).
\]

A candidate layer is a typed deformation

\[
\mathcal M_{r+1}
=
\mathcal D_{r+1}(\mathcal M_r;\theta_{r+1}),
\]

with an explicit map, domain, codomain, parameters, constraints, and
observables. A tower is

\[
\mathcal T
=
\mathcal D_R\circ\cdots\circ\mathcal D_2\circ\mathcal D_1(\mathcal M_0).
\]

Order is content only when it is witnessed:

\[
[\mathcal D_i,\mathcal D_j](\mathcal M)
=
\mathcal D_i(\mathcal D_j(\mathcal M))
-
\mathcal D_j(\mathcal D_i(\mathcal M))
\ne 0,
\]

where subtraction is replaced by a typed discriminator if the codomains are
not vector spaces.

Nonassociativity is a different claim:

\[
[\mathcal D_i,\mathcal D_j,\mathcal D_k](\mathcal M)
=
(\mathcal D_i\circ\mathcal D_j)\circ\mathcal D_k(\mathcal M)
-
\mathcal D_i\circ(\mathcal D_j\circ\mathcal D_k)(\mathcal M).
\]

The audit produced a nonzero matrix commutator with zero matrix associator,
and a separate octonion associator witness:

\[
[e_1,e_2,e_4]=2e_7.
\]

Thus noncommutation does not imply nonassociativity.

At each rung define the surviving observational quotient

\[
Q_r
=
\operatorname{Ext}_r/\!\sim_{\Pi_r},
\qquad
H_0^{(r)}=\log_2|Q_r|.
\]

The sequence \(H_0^{(r)}\) need not be monotonically decreasing. A constraint
may reduce support, while a declared frame-growth or refinement map may add
distinguishable addresses. Direction must be reported per deformation rather
than forced into a single scalar story.

### Layer admission

A deformation should be admitted only if all applicable conditions hold:

| Condition | Executable question |
|---|---|
| Finitude | Are the carrier, domains, and search bounds declared? |
| Distinction preservation | Does the candidate keep every demanded edge separated? |
| Nested comparability | Do rivals share the same parent chain and nesting witness? |
| Continuation congruence | Do merged states retain equivalent admitted continuations? |
| Deletion witness | Does removing the deformation change an admitted observable? |
| Gauge control | Can a relabeling, conjugation, or coordinate change reproduce it? |
| Order witness | Does a rival order change the typed result? |
| Independent execution | Can another implementation reproduce the declared observables? |

The live ConstraintBox Ratchet was fed engine-field observations. It retained
the coarsest sufficient nested probe prefix \(P_3\), kept \(P_4\) as a finer
survivor, and explicitly made no absolute MSS claim.

## 3. Residual degrees of freedom

For smooth equality constraints \(C_a(x)=0\) at a regular point,

\[
T_x\mathcal M_r
=
\bigcap_a\ker dC_a(x),
\qquad
\dim T_x\mathcal M_r
=
\dim X-\operatorname{rank}J_C(x).
\]

This formula does not apply unchanged to discrete spaces, singular points, or
inequality boundaries; there one needs a tangent cone, adjacency complex, or
finite transition relation.

Executed counterexample to “tightest means engine”:

| Constraints at \((1,0)\subset\mathbb R^2\) | Residual dimension |
|---|---:|
| \(x^2+y^2=1\) | 1 |
| \(x^2+y^2=1,\ y=0\) | 0 |

Tightening removed the cyclic degree of freedom. A deepest survivor can be a
point, a discrete set, a tree, or a cyclic space. Cyclic mechanics require an
additional witness.

## 4. Minimum mathematical engine contract

Let the phase-extended local state be

\[
\mathcal Y=\mathcal B\times\mathbb Z_m,
\qquad
F(x,q)=\bigl(F_q(x),q+1\bmod m\bigr).
\]

The \(\mathbb Z_m\) coordinate is the stroke phase. Without it, a list of
different stage maps is a time-dependent external schedule, not an autonomous
state law.

A candidate engine cycle must satisfy:

1. **Closure**

   \[
   F^m(x_*,q_*)=(x_*,q_*).
   \]

2. **Directed admissibility**: every transition is allowed in its orientation.

3. **Reachability**: the cycle has a nonempty basin under the same dynamics.

4. **Nontrivial stage action**: at least one \(F_q(x)\ne x\).

5. **Order content**

   \[
   F_{m-1}\circ\cdots\circ F_0
   \ne
   F_0\circ\cdots\circ F_{m-1}
   \]

   on a declared witness.

6. **Exchange or record**: a typed observable changes or a record is written.

7. **Robustness**: the cycle survives a predeclared perturbation region.

8. **Controller honesty**: report whether phase progression is externally
   imposed, internally stored, or selected by feedback.

An undirected triangle has \(\beta_1=1\), but the orientation
\(0\to1,0\to2,1\to2\) is acyclic. The hostile control demonstrated that
topological cycle rank alone is insufficient.

## 5. Gradient, circulation, and the gravity claim

For

\[
\dot x=-M\nabla V(x)+J\nabla V(x),
\qquad
M=M^\mathsf T\succeq0,\quad J=-J^\mathsf T,
\]

\[
\frac{dV}{dt}
=
-\nabla V^\mathsf T M\nabla V
\le 0.
\]

The skew term contributes no change to \(V\). If \(M\succ0\) away from critical
points, a nonconstant periodic orbit is impossible because \(V\) strictly
decreases.

The executed two-dimensional controls gave:

| Dynamics | Result after \(4\pi\) |
|---|---:|
| \(\dot x=-x\) | no return; potential drops |
| \(\dot x=Jx\) | return error \(1.14\times10^{-15}\); potential constant |
| \(\dot x=(-I+J)x\) | no return; inward spiral |

Therefore entropy-like descent plus circulation does not by itself generate a
sustained engine. A sustained autonomous cycle needs at least one additional
mechanism: a neutral direction, state-dependent drive, feedback, switching,
delay, nonautonomous forcing made internal through a phase variable, or a
non-gradient conserved structure.

No equation tested here identifies \(V\), Hartley capacity, or relative
entropy with gravitational curvature. “Entropy and gravity are one” remains
a proposed bridge requiring its own field equations, units, observables, and
falsifiers.

## 6. Quantum four-stroke scout

The bounded quantum scout used:

1. an \(x\)-axis unitary channel;
2. a partial \(z\)-pinching channel;
3. a \(z\)-axis unitary channel;
4. an amplitude-damping channel.

All outputs remained Hermitian, positive, and trace one.

| Observable | Result |
|---|---:|
| Forward/reverse order trace distance | 0.0558163 |
| Stroboscopic fixed residual after convergence | 0 |
| Maximum sampled contraction ratio | 0.816921 |
| Exact Bloch-map contraction norm | 0.820000 |
| Unitariy-stroke entropy errors | \(0,\ 1.11\times10^{-16}\) |
| Limiting phase entropies | 0.861729 → 0.861729 → 0.905691 → 0.905691 → 0.861729 bits |

Interpretation:

- order is load-bearing;
- unitary strokes are isospectral;
- dissipative strokes change the spectrum;
- the one-cycle channel is contractive and has one fixed point;
- the four distinct intermediate states form a cycle only in the
  phase-extended system.

This supports an **Otto-like channel grammar**—alternating isospectral and
spectrum-changing strokes—but it does not compute heat, work, temperature, or
efficiency.

The record-feedback scout began in \(|+\rangle\), measured in the \(z\) basis,
and conditionally reset the system:

| Observable | Result |
|---|---:|
| Record entropy | 1 bit |
| Unread post-measurement system entropy | 1 bit |
| Conditional-feedback system entropy | \(3.2\times10^{-16}\) bits |

This supports a **Szilard-like information grammar** requiring an explicit
record and feedback. No erasure cost or work extraction was modeled.

No Carnot-like claim was tested. It would require declared reservoirs,
temperatures, quasi-static paths, heat, work, and an efficiency comparison.

## 7. Hopf/ring geometry

The tested chart was

\[
\psi(\eta,\phi,\chi)
=
\begin{pmatrix}
\cos\eta\,e^{i(\phi+\chi)}\\
\sin\eta\,e^{i(\phi-\chi)}
\end{pmatrix}.
\]

At fixed \(\eta\), \((\phi,\chi)\) parameterize a torus. Discrete parallel
transport around the \(\chi\)-cycle produced:

| Observable | Result |
|---|---:|
| \(\eta_{\rm inner}\) | 0.31 |
| \(\eta_{\rm outer}\) | 0.89 |
| Relative holonomy | -0.135462217 rad |
| Analytic value | -0.135462071 rad |
| Conjugate relative holonomy | +0.135462217 rad |
| Flattened relative holonomy | 0 |

The numerical/analytic discrepancy is \(1.46\times10^{-7}\) rad.

This establishes the local Hopf-chart calculation and its controls. It does
not establish that the finite nested checkerboard deforms into this chart.
The missing bridge is an explicit sequence of finite complexes and maps whose
limit-free observables converge to the tested connection/holonomy while
preserving declared distinctions.

## 8. Deterministic subspaces and finite computation

A deterministic subspace of an engine or engine field is an invariant finite
set

\[
\mathcal D\subseteq\mathcal Y,
\qquad
F(\mathcal D)\subseteq\mathcal D,
\]

on which \(F\) is single-valued.

A finite Turing-machine instance is a finite transition system once tape
length, alphabet, head positions, and control states are bounded. The audit
enumerated 72 configurations and verified for every configuration \(c\):

\[
\operatorname{decode}\!\left(
T_{\rm table}(\operatorname{encode}(c))
\right)
=
T_{\rm TM}(c).
\]

This is an exact finite simulation equivalence. It is not equivalence to an
unbounded universal Turing machine.

The Möbius-like transport law on
\(\mathbb Z_7\times\mathbb Z_2\) gave:

\[
T^7(0,0)=(0,1),
\qquad
T^{14}(0,0)=(0,0).
\]

This is a valid orientation/track-flip boundary condition. The audit found no
reason it is required rather than one optional finite carrier.

A finite “oracle” was implemented as a set-valued relation

\[
\mathcal O(x)=\{y\in\mathbb Z_{64}:y^2=x\pmod{64}\}.
\]

It required 4,096 checks to construct by enumeration. Set-valued release is
well-defined, but no computational shortcut was earned.

## 9. Geometry and entropy beyond one engine

For a field of \(N\) local phase variables,

\[
q=(q_1,\ldots,q_N)\in\mathbb Z_4^N,
\]

the audit used the explicit ring-coupled rule

\[
q_i'
=
q_i+s_i+\mathbf 1[q_{i-1}=q_{i+1}]
\pmod 4.
\]

This rule is only a probe, not a proposed law of nature. Its functional graph
on \(4^4=256\) states generated higher-level basin geometry:

| Orientation assignment | Attractors | Cycle lengths | Largest basin | \(\log_2\) attractor count |
|---|---:|---|---:|---:|
| \((+,+,+,+)\) | 68 | 8 of length 2; 60 of length 4 | 4 | 6.08746 |
| \((+,-,+,-)\) | 68 | 8 of length 2; 60 of length 4 | 4 | 6.08746 |
| \((+,+,-,-)\) | 40 | 16 of length 4; 24 of length 8 | 8 | 5.32193 |

The uniform and alternating assignments are indistinguishable under these
observables. The paired split is distinguishable. This is a concrete gauge
warning: a visually different orientation pattern need not be a new degree of
freedom.

Once a field is defined, new typed objects become available:

| Object | Geometry | Information quantity |
|---|---|---|
| Functional graph | directed basins and cycles | \(\log_2\) number of states/attractors |
| Path ensemble | directed path complex | Shannon entropy only after path weights exist |
| Record register | simplex of outcome distributions | \(H(R)\), mutual information |
| Joint engine state | tensor-product or finite product carrier | cut entropies after a cut is declared |
| Invariant deterministic subset | finite transition graph | state complexity / Myhill–Nerode classes |
| History-pair field | matrix indexed by \((h,h')\) | diagonal probabilities and off-diagonal coherence |

These quantities extend beyond a single engine without being added into one
untyped scalar.

## 10. What was actually run

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| Existing ConstraintBox runtime | 35/35 tests pass |
| New manifold/engine audit | 11/11 tests pass |
| Hostile controls | 5/5 fired |
| Independent discrete implementation | Node and Python agree exactly |
| New test wall time | 0.659 seconds |

Hostile controls:

1. flatten nested leaves;
2. erase conjugation;
3. replace stages with commuting channels;
4. present an undirected cycle with an acyclic orientation;
5. call the Ratchet with no demand edges.

Environment limits:

- available: Python 3.12.13, NumPy 2.3.5, SciPy 1.17.0, Node 24.14.0;
- unavailable: Julia, JAX, Z3, cvc5, PySINDy, PyTorch, QuTiP, GUDHI,
  TopoNetX.

Therefore the quantum and holonomy results are single-scientific-runtime
scouts. Only the discrete results have a second-language reproduction.

## 11. Decisive next experiment

The next run should not start from the sixteen engine stages. It should test
whether cycles are selected from the finite base.

1. Declare a small deformation library

   \[
   \{\text{quotient},\text{weight},\text{complex carrier},
   \text{cut},\text{grading},\text{connection},\text{channel},\text{record}\}.
   \]

2. Generate bounded deformation words of depth \(R\), retaining their
   parentheses.

3. For every word, construct the resulting finite carrier, probe quotient,
   transition relation, residual tangent/adjacency object, and typed entropy
   vector.

4. Reject:

   - collapsed demanded distinctions;
   - non-congruent merges;
   - gauge-equivalent deformations;
   - cycles requiring an undeclared external clock;
   - undirected but nontraversable cycles;
   - cyclic schedules with no exchange or record;
   - nonassociativity claims reproduced in an associative control.

5. Admit an engine candidate only if an autonomous directed cycle survives
   deletion, order, perturbation, and independent-execution controls.

6. Feed only comparable nested survivors to the existing Ratchet. Retain
   incomparable towers; do not force a single winner or an absolute MSS.

This exact finite tournament can run on CPU first. A GPU becomes useful only
after the deformation operators and observables are fixed, when the number of
towers or field states becomes the bottleneck.

## Preserved evidence or audit chapter — Independent engine-emergence deep audit

> Preserved in full. Status labels and conflicts remain visible; this volume does not silently promote proposals into results.

# Deep audit of “Engines as DOFs”

**Status:** bounded audit of the pasted argument and attached files  
**Promotion:** `promotion_allowed: false`

## Verdict

Claude’s response contains a substantially better candidate layout than the
earlier engine story. Its most useful corrections survive:

- a monotone Ratchet cannot itself be a cyclic engine;
- an engine needs an autonomous recurrent transition structure;
- undirected cycle rank is insufficient;
- a shared invariant subspace can collapse an order-sensitive word to zero
  circulation;
- Möbius-like monodromy is a real finite carrier option;
- finite machine/rewrite conjugacy is exact when encoding and decoding are
  declared.

Four central statements remain overclaims:

1. available capacity and survivor capacity are not yet two reservoirs;
2. noncommutation is neither identical to curl nor necessary and sufficient
   for stationary circulation;
3. a nontrivial stabilizer neither forces nor is required for engine dynamics;
4. torus-leaf cohomology permits harmonic currents but does not make engines
   emerge or establish a global current on the entire Hopf carrier.

The correct surviving claim is:

> Deep ratcheting may leave a residual finite configuration space, residual
> transformations, or both. Engines exist only if the same admitted tower also
> supplies autonomous transition dynamics with a recurrent directed class and
> nonzero current. Residual symmetry proposes possible motions; it does not
> select or drive them.

## 1. Attachment integrity

All four attached scripts were rerun.

| Script | Reproduced result |
|---|---|
| `t1_t3_flows.py` | all printed checks pass |
| `t4_hodge.py` | all printed checks pass |
| `t5_t8_t12.py` | checks pass; Möbius chirality remains flagged |
| `t6_t7_engine.py` | prints **`[FAIL] T6a_noncommuting_engine`** but exits 0 |

The consolidated receipt contains three T6 result IDs absent from the attached
source:

- `T6a_symmetry_collapse`
- `T6a2_engine_no_shared_axis`
- `T6a3_reversal_chirality`

Meanwhile, the attached source contains four result IDs absent from the
receipt. The supplied pack therefore does not reproduce its own consolidated
T6 evidence. A gate that trusts exit status would admit the failing script.

A new reproduction using explicit physical channel substeps confirmed the
intended later result:

| Word | Order gap | Closed-path area norm |
|---|---:|---:|
| shared invariant axis | 0.105000 | 0 |
| no shared invariant axis | 0.774521 | 0.042684 |
| reversed no-shared-axis word | — | 0.077407 |

The forward/reverse area-vector dot product is \(-0.003265\), so their
orientations oppose. Every intermediate density operator remained positive
and trace one.

## 2. Capacity variables are not yet reservoirs

Let

\[
A_t^{\mathrm{field}}
\]

be the finite field of available configurations, and

\[
A_t^{\mathrm{surv}}
=
\{x\in A_t^{\mathrm{field}}:C_t(x)\}
\]

the subset satisfying current constraints. Then

\[
C_{\mathrm{field}}(t)=\log_2|A_t^{\mathrm{field}}|,
\qquad
C_{\mathrm{surv}}(t)=\log_2|A_t^{\mathrm{surv}}|.
\]

These are two typed capacity measurements, but the second is computed from a
subset of the first. They are not independent reservoirs merely because their
values differ.

To earn reservoir-like mechanics, the model needs explicit balance laws, for
example:

\[
C_f(t+1)-C_f(t)=I_t-T_t,
\]

\[
C_s(t+1)-C_s(t)=T_t-E_t,
\]

with independently measurable injection \(I_t\), transfer \(T_t\), and
ejection/record \(E_t\). A cyclic word must couple to these currents. Without
such maps, “two reservoirs” is metaphor rather than mechanism.

It is also false that every thermodynamic engine is a two-reservoir machine.
Szilard-style engines use a bath plus measurement, memory, and feedback;
nonthermal and autonomous engines can have different resource structures.

## 3. Noncommutation, curl, and detailed balance

For stage maps \(\Phi_i,\Phi_j\), the order defect is

\[
\Delta_{ij}
=
\|\Phi_i\Phi_j-\Phi_j\Phi_i\|.
\]

This establishes order sensitivity. It is not a curl until a state-space
connection, discrete exterior derivative, or probability-current map is
defined.

For a finite Markov process with stationary distribution \(\pi\), the current
is

\[
J_{xy}
=
\pi_xP_{xy}-\pi_yP_{yx}.
\]

Detailed balance is \(J_{xy}=0\) for every pair. Cycle affinity is

\[
\mathcal A_\gamma
=
\log\prod_{(x,y)\in\gamma}
\frac{P_{xy}}{P_{yx}}.
\]

Claude’s T7 correctly demonstrates that a biased three-state Markov cycle has
nonzero affinity and stationary current. It does not demonstrate that a
deformation commutator caused that current.

Executed counterexamples:

- noncommuting quantum stages with a shared invariant axis: order gap \(0.105\),
  current-area \(0\);
- a single Markov generator, with no pair of scheduled deformation maps,
  carries stationary current \(1/3\).

Therefore noncommutation is neither sufficient nor necessary in general. It
can be a model-specific source of current only after a controlled ablation
shows:

\[
\Delta_{ij}\to0
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad
J\to0
\]

while the carrier, drive, stationary distribution, and other couplings are
held fixed.

## 4. Hodge decomposition: what it earns

On a declared finite cell complex, an edge flow can be decomposed as

\[
j=j_{\mathrm{exact}}+j_{\mathrm{coexact}}+h,
\qquad
h\in\ker\Delta_1.
\]

The attached calculations correctly recover:

\[
b_1(S^2)=0,
\qquad
b_1(T^2)=2.
\]

This means:

- a torus supports two independent harmonic one-form classes;
- a sphere has no nonzero harmonic one-form;
- local coexact eddies and driven limit cycles can still exist on
  contractible spaces;
- a harmonic component is relevant to homology-class or reroute-invariant
  current, not to engine existence in general.

There is an additional nesting boundary:

\[
H^1(T_\eta)\cong\mathbb Z^2,
\qquad
H^1(S^3)=0.
\]

Each fixed-\(\eta\) Hopf torus has two leafwise cycles, but the full Hopf
carrier \(S^3\) has no global first cohomology. A leafwise harmonic current is
not automatically a global harmonic current. The model must test:

1. whether the dynamics remains confined to \(T_\eta\);
2. how currents transport when \(\eta\) changes;
3. whether leafwise classes survive the enclosing geometry;
4. whether the measured phase is curvature holonomy, harmonic current, or
   both.

The Hodge script contains no Hopf connection and does not prove “flux requires
nesting.” It tests homological rerouting on a triangulated torus.

## 5. Residual symmetry is not engine dynamics

The following objects must remain separate:

| Object | Formal definition | What it provides |
|---|---|---|
| Survivor set | \(A_r=\{x:C_r(x)\}\) | configurations |
| Residual tangent/kernel | \(\ker C_r\) or \(\bigcap\ker dC_i\) | permitted perturbations |
| Stabilizer | \(G_r=\operatorname{Aut}_{C_r,\Pi_r}(A_r)\) | structure-preserving relabelings |
| Transition law | \(P_r,\ F_r,\) or \(\Phi_r\) | actual dynamics |
| Recurrent class | closed communicating component of \(P_r\) | repeated reachability |
| Current | \(J_{xy}\) | directed circulation |

None of the first three supplies the fourth.

In T8, every nonzero null-space vector is explicitly installed as an allowed
move. The resulting Cayley graph cycles because the test authors those moves.
It shows that residual translations *can carry* cycles, not that the Ratchet
generates them.

Two new finite countertests make the logical boundary explicit:

- residual group \(\mathbb Z_4\) plus identity dynamics: no nonconstant cycle;
- a labeled three-state directed cycle with a trivial probe-preserving
  automorphism group: a cycle exists.

Thus “engine iff nontrivial stabilizer” is false. A stabilizer can be a
candidate generator library, but engine admission requires an independently
earned transition law and current.

## 6. Correct engine-emergence contract

At a candidate stall \(r_*\), define:

\[
\mathcal E_{r_*}
=
(A_{r_*},\Pi_{r_*},F_{r_*},R_{r_*},\mathcal O_{r_*}).
\]

An engine is earned only if:

1. \(A_{r_*}\) survives the declared constraint packet;
2. \(F_{r_*}\) is derived from admitted deformation rules rather than inserted
   as a desired schedule;
3. \(F_{r_*}\) has a reachable recurrent directed class;
4. that class contains a nonconstant cycle;
5. the steady edge current is nonzero;
6. at least one typed exchange, record, or work-like observable changes;
7. the phase/controller is internal to the state;
8. deleting or commuting a claimed load-bearing stage changes the current;
9. the cycle survives a declared perturbation region;
10. gauge-equivalent orientations are not counted as separate engines.

For a Markov engine, use:

\[
\sigma
=
\frac12\sum_{x,y}
J_{xy}
\log\frac{\pi_xP_{xy}}{\pi_yP_{yx}}
\ge0
\]

when the hypotheses for stochastic entropy production hold.

For a quantum channel word, distinguish:

- stroboscopic fixed state of the full channel;
- intermediate phase orbit of the scheduled strokes;
- geometric phase/holonomy;
- probability or entropy current;
- energy/work current, if a Hamiltonian and reservoirs are eventually
  licensed.

These are not interchangeable cycle witnesses.

## 7. Stroke-role audit

Claude’s role table is a useful candidate mapping, not a derivation.

| Formal map | What is valid | What is not yet earned |
|---|---|---|
| \(\rho\mapsto U\rho U^\dagger\) | deterministic and reversible on density operators; \(S(\rho)\) constant | zero physical cost; unique identification with one engine region |
| Instrument \(\{\mathcal I_y\}\) | outcome probabilities and conditional states | “only stochastic role” |
| Pinching \(\rho\mapsto\sum_jP_j\rho P_j\) | deterministic unread channel; irreversible unless already block diagonal | a written record without a record register |
| Dissipative semigroup \(e^{t\mathcal L}\) | deterministic density evolution | globally fixed entropy sign; literal erasure cost without a thermal model |

Amplitude damping can increase and then decrease von Neumann entropy,
depending on the initial state. A stochastic unraveling of the same master
equation also produces stochastic trajectories. “Read/write/move/erase” must
remain interpretation labels outside the formal equations until deletion and
record tests earn them.

## 8. Thermodynamic analogue audit

| Analogue | Current value | Boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Otto | useful four-channel grammar: isospectral/dissipative alternation | no Hamiltonian-gap work, Gibbs contacts, heat, or efficiency |
| Szilard | useful instrument–record–feedback grammar | Landauer cost requires a thermal bath and units not present at the Hartley floor |
| Carnot | currently weak | two capacity values do not create hot/cold reservoirs |

In T6c, the entropy changes sum to zero because the periodic state returns to
itself:

\[
\sum_k\Delta S_k=S(\rho_{\rm final})-S(\rho_{\rm initial})=0.
\]

That is true for every state function on a closed state cycle. It is not by
itself a two-reservoir signature.

T10 builds

\[
\Delta S=\lambda I,\qquad 0\le\lambda\le1,
\]

so \(\Delta S\le I\) holds by construction. It is a positive algebra check,
not an independent demon simulation. Typed bookkeeping makes a proper audit
possible; it does not automatically satisfy the full energy/information
balance.

## 9. Möbius and spinor structures

The finite transport

\[
T^L(i,s)=(i,1-s),
\qquad
T^{2L}(i,s)=(i,s)
\]

is reproduced. With a \(\mathbb Z_2\) fibre there is one nontrivial monodromy
class, not separate left and right twists.

Four structures must not be identified:

| Structure | Map |
|---|---|
| antiperiodic phase | \(\psi\mapsto-\psi\) |
| sheet exchange | \(L\leftrightarrow R\) |
| complex conjugation | \(\psi\mapsto\bar\psi\) |
| orientation reversal | base-cycle direction reversed |

Spinor \(4\pi\) periodicity comes from the double cover
\(\operatorname{Spin}(3)\to SO(3)\). A Möbius line bundle has nontrivial first
Stiefel–Whitney class. They can share a “return after two traversals” pattern,
but they are not the same mathematical object.

A meaningful bundle tournament should test each monodromy separately and
only compose them through declared maps.

## 10. Finite computation and tape

The attached T12 result is valid:

\[
E\circ F_{\rm TM}=R\circ E
\]

for the finite toy carrier. This proves conjugacy between two
representations—TM configuration and marked-word rewrite—not identity between
machine and tape, and not a fixed point

\[
\Theta(M)=M.
\]

Feynman/Benioff constructions establish that reversible computational
histories can be embedded in Hamiltonian dynamics. They do not establish that
one proposed engine region is naturally a universal computer, nor that
initialization, readout, and error correction are free.

## 11. Enumeration result

The supplied receipt reports approximately 4.2 hours for the toy
\(4^{16}=2^{32}\) sweep. Rerunning the attached script here produced
approximately 0.3 hours because its vectorized kernel measured about
210 ns/assignment.

This instability is expected: the benchmark evaluates a tiny additive table
and neighbor term, not manifold settlement. It excludes candidate generation,
memory transfer, full constraints, basin iteration, and receipts. The only
valid conclusion is Claude’s final qualification:

> Measure real settlement cost before choosing enumeration or factorization.

## 12. The decisive emergence experiment

Begin with the finite nested support and deformation tower. Do **not** insert
an antisymmetric matrix, biased Markov cycle, phase clock, or four-stroke word.

For every surviving tower:

1. derive its transition law from the same admitted local constraints;
2. compute the empirical stationary distribution and edge current;
3. decompose current into exact, coexact, and harmonic components;
4. identify recurrent directed classes;
5. test whether phase progression is internal;
6. ablate deformation commutators without changing other data;
7. compute the residual automorphism group separately;
8. compare whether current tracks the group, the drive, both, or neither;
9. run the cylinder/antiperiodic/sheet-exchange/conjugation bundle rivals;
10. feed only nested, probe-compatible candidates to the Ratchet.

The model’s central engine claim survives only if nonzero autonomous current
appears without being authored into the transition law.

## Authority rule

The owner’s current explicit instructions outrank generated manuals, repository “current” labels, branch names, old versions, wiki content, Gemini/Claude/Codex prose, schemas, and committed receipts.

Generated material may identify a conflict or propose a branch. It may not decide an owner-reserved question through repetition, polish, a filename such as `BINDING`, or an apparently formal schema.

## Critical non-collapse checksum

- LevOS is separate and must not be modified.
- ClaimGate is an external bridge/control plane and must fail closed.
- Codex-Ratchet versions must remain separate; the owner calls the current project v8, but this audit did not locate one coherent v8 root.
- Julia is the reference semantics lane.
- JAX is the numerical workhorse.
- NumPy is satellite/support, not the primary workhorse.
- PyTorch is support/training where specifically earned.
- The Ratchet is deterministic and comparison-based; LLMs may propose but cannot settle MSS or ratchet root constraints.
- MSS is not generic Pareto sorting.
- Geometry and entropy must be tested as one co-ratcheting nested structure, not two late-joined tracks.
- Ring-checkerboard/nested shells and spinor/chirality/global orientation must not be silently demoted to late visualization features.
- Axis 0 is a whole-manifold entropy–geometry gradient/cofield role; any exact formula remains candidate until typed and earned.
- Deduction: `Ne → Si → Se → Ni`.
- Induction: `Ne → Ni → Se → Si`.
- Type 1: outer Deduction, inner Induction.
- Type 2: outer Induction, inner Deduction.
- Outer-to-inner state and record handoff is unreset.
- There are 16 ordered engine positions and four rival candidate bindings at each position: 64 candidate cells, not 64 sequential stages.
- IGT uses `WINwin`, `WINlose`, `LOSEwin`, and `LOSElose` without moralizing WIN/LOSE.
- GPU, annealing, tensor networks, and formal solvers are bounded tools, not proof or truth oracles.
- A finite namesake does not solve an all-input, infinite, continuum, or theorem-level problem.
